// ==========================================
// File: src/components/stay/KakaoMap.tsx
// ==========================================
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Plus, Minus } from 'lucide-react';
import type { Stay } from '../../types/stay.ts';
import { money, getLodgingTypeName } from '../../data/stays.ts';

declare const window: typeof globalThis & {
    kakao: any;
};

interface KakaoStayMapProps {
    stays: Stay[];
    level?: number;
    height?: string;
    mode?: 'search' | 'detail';
    zoomable?: boolean; // 마우스 휠 확대/축소 허용 여부 (기본값: true)
}

export function KakaoStayMap({
                                 stays,
                                 level,
                                 height = '520px',
                                 mode = 'search',
                                 zoomable = true
                             }: KakaoStayMapProps) {
    const mapContainerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
    const mapInstanceRef = useRef<any>(null); // +/- 버튼 제어용 지도 인스턴스 보관
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const [isLoaded, setIsLoaded] = useState(false);

    // 1. 카카오 지도 SDK 동적 주입
    useEffect(() => {
        let isMounted = true;

        const initKakaoMap = async () => {
            const existingScript = document.getElementById('kakao-map-sdk');
            if (existingScript) {
                if (window.kakao && window.kakao.maps) {
                    if (isMounted) setIsLoaded(true);
                } else {
                    existingScript.addEventListener('load', () => {
                        if (isMounted) setIsLoaded(true);
                    });
                }
                return;
            }

            let appKey = import.meta.env.VITE_KAKAO_MAP_KEY || '';
            if (!appKey) {
                try {
                    const res = await fetch('/api/v1/accommodations/map-key');
                    if (res.ok) {
                        const data = await res.json();
                        appKey = data.mapKey;
                    }
                } catch (e) {
                    console.error('카카오 지도 키를 가져오지 못했습니다.', e);
                }
            }

            if (!appKey || !isMounted) return;

            const script = document.createElement('script');
            script.id = 'kakao-map-sdk';
            script.src = `//dapi.kakao.com/v2/maps/sdk.js?appkey=${appKey}&libraries=clusterer,services&autoload=false`;
            script.async = true;

            script.onload = () => {
                if (isMounted) setIsLoaded(true);
            };

            document.head.appendChild(script);
        };

        initKakaoMap();

        return () => {
            isMounted = false;
        };
    }, []);

    // 2. 지도 초기화 및 마커 렌더링
    useEffect(() => {
        if (!isLoaded || !window.kakao || !window.kakao.maps) return;

        window.kakao.maps.load(() => {
            if (!mapContainerRef.current) return;

            const validStays = stays.filter(
                (s) => s.mapX && s.mapY && !isNaN(Number(s.mapX)) && !isNaN(Number(s.mapY))
            );

            const isDetailPage = mode === 'detail';
            const isSingleStay = validStays.length === 1;

            const centerPosition = (isDetailPage || isSingleStay) && validStays.length > 0
                ? new window.kakao.maps.LatLng(
                    parseFloat(validStays[0].mapY!),
                    parseFloat(validStays[0].mapX!)
                )
                : new window.kakao.maps.LatLng(33.3617, 126.5292);

            const zoomLevel = level ?? (isDetailPage ? 4 : isSingleStay ? 6 : 9);

            const options = {
                center: centerPosition,
                level: zoomLevel,
            };
            const map = new window.kakao.maps.Map(mapContainerRef.current, options);
            mapInstanceRef.current = map;

            // 마우스 휠 확대/축소 제어 (false일 때 스크롤 내려도 지도 축척이 변하지 않음)
            map.setZoomable(zoomable);

            // [케이스 A] 객실 상세페이지 전용 뷰 (mode === 'detail') -> detailCard, centerPosition 사용
            if (isDetailPage && validStays.length > 0) {
                const stay = validStays[0];
                const marker = new window.kakao.maps.Marker({
                    position: centerPosition,
                    map: map,
                });

                const detailCard = document.createElement('div');
                detailCard.style.cssText = `
                    background: #ffffff;
                    border-radius: 12px;
                    box-shadow: 0 4px 18px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.16);
                    border: 1px solid #cbd5e1;
                    padding: 12px 14px;
                    min-width: 220px;
                    max-width: 280px;
                    font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", Roboto, sans-serif;
                    position: relative;
                    z-index: 9999;
                `;

                const uid = `${stay.contentId}-${zoomable ? 'popup' : 'inline'}`;

                detailCard.innerHTML = `
                    <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: flex-start; margin-bottom: 2px;">
                        <span style="font-size: 11px; color: #0f766e; font-weight: 700;">
                            ${getLodgingTypeName(stay.lodgingType)}
                        </span>
                        <button id="btn-close-detail-overlay-${uid}" style="
                            background: none;
                            border: none;
                            font-size: 14px;
                            color: #94a3b8;
                            cursor: pointer;
                            padding: 0 0 0 8px;
                            line-height: 1;
                        ">✕</button>
                    </div>
                    <strong style="display: block; font-size: 14px; color: #0f172a; margin-bottom: 4px; line-height: 1.3;">
                        ${stay.title}
                    </strong>
                    <div style="font-size: 11px; color: #64748b; line-height: 1.4; margin-bottom: 8px; word-break: keep-all;">
                        ${stay.addr || '등록된 주소 정보 없음'}
                    </div>
                    <div style="display: flex; gap: 6px;">
                        <button id="btn-copy-stay-addr-${uid}" style="
                            flex: 1;
                            padding: 6px 0;
                            background-color: #f1f5f9;
                            color: #123f4d;
                            border: 1px solid #cbd5e1;
                            border-radius: 6px;
                            font-size: 11px;
                            font-weight: 600;
                            cursor: pointer;
                        ">
                            주소 복사
                        </button>
                        <button id="btn-close-bottom-${uid}" style="
                            padding: 6px 12px;
                            background-color: #ffffff;
                            color: #64748b;
                            border: 1px solid #cbd5e1;
                            border-radius: 6px;
                            font-size: 11px;
                            cursor: pointer;
                        ">
                            닫기
                        </button>
                    </div>
                `;

                const overlay = new window.kakao.maps.CustomOverlay({
                    content: detailCard,
                    position: centerPosition,
                    yAnchor: 1.3,
                    zIndex: 9999,
                    clickable: true,
                });

                overlay.setMap(map);

                window.kakao.maps.event.addListener(marker, 'click', () => {
                    overlay.setMap(map);
                });

                setTimeout(() => {
                    const copyBtn = document.getElementById(`btn-copy-stay-addr-${uid}`);
                    const closeXBtn = document.getElementById(`btn-close-detail-overlay-${uid}`);
                    const closeBottomBtn = document.getElementById(`btn-close-bottom-${uid}`);

                    if (copyBtn && stay.addr) {
                        copyBtn.onclick = (e) => {
                            e.stopPropagation();
                            navigator.clipboard.writeText(stay.addr as string);
                            alert('주소가 클립보드에 복사되었습니다.');
                        };
                    }
                    if (closeXBtn) {
                        closeXBtn.onclick = (e) => {
                            e.stopPropagation();
                            overlay.setMap(null);
                        };
                    }
                    if (closeBottomBtn) {
                        closeBottomBtn.onclick = (e) => {
                            e.stopPropagation();
                            overlay.setMap(null);
                        };
                    }
                }, 0);

                return;
            }

            // [케이스 B] 검색/홈페이지 지도 모드 (mode === 'search') -> content, position 사용
            const clusterer = new window.kakao.maps.MarkerClusterer({
                map: map,
                averageCenter: true,
                minLevel: 6,
                disableClickZoom: false,
            });

            let currentOverlay: any = null;
            let currentActiveMarker: any = null;

            const closeCurrentOverlay = () => {
                if (currentOverlay) {
                    currentOverlay.setMap(null);
                    currentOverlay = null;
                }
                if (currentActiveMarker) {
                    currentActiveMarker.setZIndex(1);
                    currentActiveMarker = null;
                }
            };

            window.kakao.maps.event.addListener(map, 'click', closeCurrentOverlay);
            window.kakao.maps.event.addListener(map, 'zoom_start', closeCurrentOverlay);

            const markers = validStays.map((stay) => {
                const lat = parseFloat(stay.mapY!);
                const lng = parseFloat(stay.mapX!);
                const position = new window.kakao.maps.LatLng(lat, lng);

                const marker = new window.kakao.maps.Marker({
                    position: position,
                    zIndex: 1,
                });

                const content = document.createElement('div');
                content.style.cssText = `
                    background: #ffffff;
                    border-radius: 14px;
                    box-shadow: 0 10px 28px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.25);
                    border: 1.5px solid #0f766e;
                    padding: 14px 16px;
                    width: 230px;
                    position: relative;
                    z-index: 9999;
                    font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", Roboto, sans-serif;
                `;

                content.onmousedown = (e) => e.stopPropagation();
                content.onclick = (e) => e.stopPropagation();

                content.innerHTML = `
                    <div style="font-size: 11px; color: #0f766e; font-weight: 700; margin-bottom: 2px;">
                        ${getLodgingTypeName(stay.lodgingType)}
                    </div>
                    <strong style="display: block; font-size: 14px; color: #0f172a; margin-bottom: 4px; white-space: nowrap; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis;">
                        ${stay.title}
                    </strong>
                    <div style="font-size: 11px; color: #64748b; line-height: 1.3; margin-bottom: 8px; white-space: nowrap; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis;">
                        ${stay.addr || ''}
                    </div>
                    <div style="font-size: 13px; font-weight: 700; color: #123f4d; margin-bottom: 10px;">
                        ${money(stay.basePrice)} <span style="font-size: 11px; font-weight: 400; color: #94a3b8;">/ 1박</span>
                    </div>
                    <div style="display: flex; gap: 6px;">
                        <button id="btn-detail-${stay.contentId}" style="
                            flex: 1;
                            padding: 7px 0;
                            background-color: #123f4d;
                            color: #ffffff;
                            border: none;
                            border-radius: 6px;
                            font-size: 12px;
                            font-weight: 600;
                            cursor: pointer;
                        ">
                            상세보기
                        </button>
                        <button id="btn-close-${stay.contentId}" style="
                            padding: 7px 10px;
                            background-color: #f1f5f9;
                            color: #475569;
                            border: 1px solid #cbd5e1;
                            border-radius: 6px;
                            font-size: 12px;
                            cursor: pointer;
                        ">
                            닫기
                        </button>
                    </div>
                `;

                const overlay = new window.kakao.maps.CustomOverlay({
                    content: content,
                    position: position,
                    yAnchor: 1.25,
                    zIndex: 9999, // 다른 숙소 핀/클러스터보다 항상 앞에 표시
                    clickable: true,
                });

                const showOverlay = () => {
                    closeCurrentOverlay();
                    marker.setZIndex(100);
                    overlay.setZIndex(9999);
                    overlay.setMap(map);
                    currentOverlay = overlay;
                    currentActiveMarker = marker;

                    setTimeout(() => {
                        const detailBtn = document.getElementById(`btn-detail-${stay.contentId}`);
                        const closeBtn = document.getElementById(`btn-close-${stay.contentId}`);

                        if (detailBtn) {
                            detailBtn.onclick = (e) => {
                                e.stopPropagation();
                                navigate(`/stays/${stay.contentId}`);
                            };
                        }
                        if (closeBtn) {
                            closeBtn.onclick = (e) => {
                                e.stopPropagation();
                                closeCurrentOverlay();
                            };
                        }
                    }, 0);
                };

                window.kakao.maps.event.addListener(marker, 'click', showOverlay);

                if (isSingleStay) {
                    showOverlay();
                }

                return marker;
            });

            if (isSingleStay && markers.length > 0) {
                markers[0].setMap(map);
            } else if (markers.length > 0) {
                clusterer.addMarkers(markers);
            }
        });
    }, [isLoaded, stays, level, mode, zoomable, navigate]);

    // 우측 하단 확대(+) / 축소(-) 버튼 핸들러 (휠 잠금 상태에서도 언제든 작동)
    const handleZoomIn = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        if (mapInstanceRef.current) {
            mapInstanceRef.current.setLevel(mapInstanceRef.current.getLevel() - 1, { animate: true });
        }
    };

    const handleZoomOut = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        if (mapInstanceRef.current) {
            mapInstanceRef.current.setLevel(mapInstanceRef.current.getLevel() + 1, { animate: true });
        }
    };

    return (
        <div style={{ position: 'relative', width: '100%', height }}>
            <div
                ref={mapContainerRef}
                style={{
                    width: '100%',
                    height: '100%',
                    borderRadius: '16px',
                    border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                    overflow: 'hidden',
                    boxShadow: '0 2px 10px rgba(0,0,0,0.05)',
                    backgroundColor: '#f1f5f9',
                    display: 'flex',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                    justifyContent: 'center',
                }}
            >
                {!isLoaded && (
                    <span style={{ color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: '14px' }}>
                        지도를 불러오는 중입니다...
                    </span>
                )}
            </div>

            {/* 우측 하단 확대(+) / 축소(-) 컨트롤 버튼 */}
            {isLoaded && (
                <div
                    onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
                    style={{
                        position: 'absolute',
                        right: '14px',
                        bottom: '14px',
                        zIndex: 10,
                        display: 'flex',
                        flexDirection: 'column',
                        backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                        borderRadius: '10px',
                        boxShadow: '0 4px 12px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.15)',
                        border: '1px solid #cbd5e1',
                        overflow: 'hidden',
                    }}
                >
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={handleZoomIn}
                        title="지도 확대"
                        aria-label="지도 확대"
                        style={{
                            width: '36px',
                            height: '36px',
                            border: 'none',
                            borderBottom: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                            backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                            color: '#123f4d',
                            display: 'flex',
                            alignItems: 'center',
                            justifyContent: 'center',
                            cursor: 'pointer',
                        }}
                    >
                        <Plus size={18} strokeWidth={2.5} />
                    </button>
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={handleZoomOut}
                        title="지도 축소"
                        aria-label="지도 축소"
                        style={{
                            width: '36px',
                            height: '36px',
                            border: 'none',
                            backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                            color: '#123f4d',
                            display: 'flex',
                            alignItems: 'center',
                            justifyContent: 'center',
                            cursor: 'pointer',
                        }}
                    >
                        <Minus size={18} strokeWidth={2.5} />
                    </button>
                </div>
            )}
        </div>
    );
}


// ==========================================
// File: src/components/stay/RoomCard.tsx
// ==========================================
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { BedDouble, User, Image as ImageIcon, ImageOff } from 'lucide-react';
import { money } from '../../data/stays.ts'

export interface RoomDto {
    roomId: number;
    roomTitle: string;
    roomSize: string;
    roomBaseCount: string;
    roomMaxCount: string;
    pricePerNight: number;
    availableCount?: number;
    imageUrls: string[];
}

interface RoomCardProps {
    room: RoomDto;
    stayTitle: string;
    stayAddr: string;
    initialCheckIn?: string;
    initialCheckOut?: string;
    onPreviewImage?: (url: string) => void;
}

export function RoomCard({
                             room,
                             stayTitle,
                             stayAddr,
                             initialCheckIn,
                             initialCheckOut,
                             onPreviewImage
                         }: RoomCardProps) {
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const hasImages = room.imageUrls && room.imageUrls.length > 0;
    const [activeImage, setActiveImage] = useState<string>(hasImages ? room.imageUrls[0] : '');

    const availableCount = room.availableCount ?? 0;
    const isSoldOut = availableCount <= 0;
    const isUrgent = availableCount > 0 && availableCount <= 3;

    const handleReserveClick = () => {
        if (isSoldOut) return;

        const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
        if (!token) {
            alert('로그인이 필요한 서비스입니다.');
            navigate('/login');
            return;
        }

        navigate('/reservation', {
            state: {
                roomId: room.roomId,
                roomTitle: room.roomTitle,
                pricePerNight: room.pricePerNight,
                roomImage: hasImages ? room.imageUrls[0] : '',
                stayTitle: stayTitle,
                stayAddr: stayAddr,
                checkIn: initialCheckIn,
                checkOut: initialCheckOut,
            }
        });
    };

    useEffect(() => {
        if (hasImages) {
            setActiveImage(room.imageUrls[0]);
        } else {
            setActiveImage('');
        }
    }, [room.imageUrls, hasImages]);

    return (
        <div
            style={{
                border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                borderRadius: '16px',
                overflow: 'hidden',
                backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                boxShadow: '0 2px 8px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.04)',
                opacity: isSoldOut ? 0.75 : 1,
            }}
        >
            {hasImages ? (
                <>
                    <div
                        onClick={() => activeImage && onPreviewImage?.(activeImage)}
                        style={{
                            position: 'relative',
                            width: '100%',
                            height: '280px',
                            backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                            cursor: 'zoom-in'
                        }}
                    >
                        <img
                            src={activeImage}
                            alt={room.roomTitle}
                            style={{
                                width: '100%',
                                height: '100%',
                                objectFit: 'contain',
                                display: 'block',
                                transition: 'all 0.25s ease',
                            }}
                        />
                        {room.imageUrls.length > 1 && (
                            <div
                                style={{
                                    position: 'absolute',
                                    bottom: '12px',
                                    right: '12px',
                                    backgroundColor: 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.65)',
                                    color: '#ffffff',
                                    fontSize: '11px',
                                    padding: '4px 8px',
                                    borderRadius: '12px',
                                    display: 'flex',
                                    alignItems: 'center',
                                    gap: '4px',
                                }}
                            >
                                <ImageIcon size={12} />
                                {room.imageUrls.indexOf(activeImage) + 1} / {room.imageUrls.length}
                            </div>
                        )}
                    </div>

                    {room.imageUrls.length > 1 && (
                        <div
                            style={{
                                display: 'flex',
                                gap: '8px',
                                padding: '10px 16px',
                                backgroundColor: '#f8fafc',
                                borderBottom: '1px solid #f1f5f9',
                                overflowX: 'auto',
                            }}
                        >
                            {room.imageUrls.map((imgUrl, idx) => (
                                <button
                                    key={idx}
                                    type="button"
                                    onClick={() => setActiveImage(imgUrl)}
                                    style={{
                                        width: '64px',
                                        height: '48px',
                                        borderRadius: '6px',
                                        overflow: 'hidden',
                                        border: activeImage === imgUrl ? '2px solid #0f766e' : '1px solid #cbd5e1',
                                        padding: 0,
                                        cursor: 'pointer',
                                        flexShrink: 0,
                                        opacity: activeImage === imgUrl ? 1 : 0.65,
                                        transition: 'all 0.15s ease',
                                    }}
                                >
                                    <img
                                        src={imgUrl}
                                        alt={`thumbnail-${idx}`}
                                        style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%', objectFit: 'cover' }}
                                    />
                                </button>
                            ))}
                        </div>
                    )}
                </>
            ) : (
                <div
                    style={{
                        width: '100%',
                        height: '180px',
                        backgroundColor: '#f1f5f9',
                        borderBottom: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                        display: 'flex',
                        flexDirection: 'column',
                        alignItems: 'center',
                        justifyContent: 'center',
                        gap: '8px',
                        color: '#94a3b8',
                    }}
                >
                    <ImageOff size={28} color="#94a3b8" />
                    <span style={{ fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 500 }}>
                        객실 사진 정보를 제공하지 않는 숙소입니다.
                    </span>
                </div>
            )}

            <div style={{ padding: '20px' }}>
                <div
                    style={{
                        display: 'flex',
                        justifyContent: 'space-between',
                        alignItems: 'flex-start',
                        flexWrap: 'wrap',
                        gap: '16px',
                    }}
                >
                    <div>
                        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '10px', marginBottom: '8px' }}>
                            <h3 style={{ fontSize: '18px', color: '#0f172a', margin: 0, fontWeight: 700 }}>
                                {room.roomTitle}
                            </h3>

                            <span
                                style={{
                                    padding: '3px 10px',
                                    borderRadius: '12px',
                                    fontSize: '12px',
                                    fontWeight: 700,
                                    backgroundColor: isSoldOut
                                        ? '#f1f5f9'
                                        : isUrgent
                                            ? '#fef2f2'
                                            : '#f0fdfa',
                                    color: isSoldOut
                                        ? '#94a3b8'
                                        : isUrgent
                                            ? '#dc2626'
                                            : '#0f766e',
                                    border: `1px solid ${
                                        isSoldOut
                                            ? '#e2e8f0'
                                            : isUrgent
                                                ? '#fecaca'
                                                : '#ccfbf1'
                                    }`,
                                }}
                            >
                                {isSoldOut
                                    ? '예약 마감 (0실)'
                                    : isUrgent
                                        ? `마감임박 · 잔여 ${availableCount}실`
                                        : `잔여 ${availableCount}실`}
                            </span>
                        </div>

                        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '14px', color: '#64748b', fontSize: '13px' }}>
                            <span style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '5px' }}>
                                <BedDouble size={15} color="#0f766e" /> {room.roomSize ? room.roomSize : '기본 객실'}
                            </span>
                            <span style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '5px' }}>
                                <User size={15} color="#0f766e" /> 기준 {room.roomBaseCount || '2'}인 / 최대 {room.roomMaxCount || '4'}인
                            </span>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '20px' }}>
                        <div style={{ textAlign: 'right' }}>
                            <span style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: '#94a3b8', display: 'block' }}>1박 기준</span>
                            <strong style={{ fontSize: '22px', color: isSoldOut ? '#94a3b8' : '#0f766e' }}>
                                {money(room.pricePerNight)}
                            </strong>
                        </div>

                        <button
                            type="button"
                            disabled={isSoldOut}
                            onClick={handleReserveClick}
                            style={{
                                padding: '12px 24px',
                                backgroundColor: isSoldOut ? '#cbd5e1' : '#123f4d',
                                color: '#ffffff',
                                border: 'none',
                                borderRadius: '8px',
                                fontWeight: 600,
                                fontSize: '14px',
                                cursor: isSoldOut ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
                                transition: 'background-color 0.2s',
                            }}
                            onMouseOver={(e) => {
                                if (!isSoldOut) e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = '#0e313c';
                            }}
                            onMouseOut={(e) => {
                                if (!isSoldOut) e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = '#123f4d';
                            }}
                        >
                            {isSoldOut ? '예약 마감' : '예약하기'}
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
}


// ==========================================
// File: src/components/stay/BookingModal.tsx
// ==========================================
import { ArrowRight, MapPin, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { money, getLodgingTypeName } from '../../data/stays.ts';
import type { useReservation } from '../../hooks/useReservation.ts';

type Reservation = ReturnType<typeof useReservation>;

export function BookingModal({ reservation }: { reservation: Reservation }) {
  const { selected, close } = reservation;
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  if (!selected) return null;

  const bgImage = selected.firstImage || selected.firstImage2 || '/jeju-stays.png';

  const handleGoDetail = () => {
    close();
    navigate(`/stays/${selected.contentId}`);
  };

  return (
      <div className="modal-backdrop" onMouseDown={(e) => { if (e.target === e.currentTarget) close(); }}>
        <section className="modal" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="booking-title" style={{ maxWidth: '480px' }}>
          <button className="modal-close" onClick={close} aria-label="닫기">
            <X size={21} />
          </button>
          <div className="modal-image crop-0" style={{ backgroundImage: `url(${bgImage})`, height: '220px' }} />
          <div className="modal-body" style={{ padding: '24px' }}>
            <span className="eyebrow">JEJU STAY</span>
            <h2 id="booking-title" style={{ fontSize: '22px', margin: '4px 0 10px 0' }}>{selected.title}</h2>
            <p className="modal-address" style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '4px', color: '#64748b', fontSize: '13px' }}>
              <MapPin size={16} />
              {selected.addr} · {getLodgingTypeName(selected.lodgingType)}
            </p>
            <p style={{ fontSize: '14px', color: '#475569', lineHeight: 1.6, margin: '14px 0' }}>
              {selected.overview ? `${selected.overview.slice(0, 110)}...` : '아늑하고 편안한 제주 숙소입니다.'}
            </p>
            <div className="modal-price" style={{ margin: '16px 0 20px 0', padding: '12px 16px', background: '#f8fafc', borderRadius: '8px' }}>
              1박 최저가 <strong style={{ color: '#0f766e', fontSize: '18px', marginLeft: '6px' }}>{money(selected.basePrice)}</strong>
            </div>

            <button
                type="button"
                className="reserve-button"
                onClick={handleGoDetail}
                style={{
                  width: '100%',
                  padding: '14px',
                  backgroundColor: '#123f4d',
                  color: '#ffffff',
                  border: 'none',
                  borderRadius: '8px',
                  fontWeight: 600,
                  fontSize: '15px',
                  cursor: 'pointer',
                  display: 'flex',
                  alignItems: 'center',
                  justifyContent: 'center',
                  gap: '8px'
                }}
            >
              숙소 상세 및 객실 보러가기 <ArrowRight size={17} />
            </button>
          </div>
        </section>
      </div>
  );
}


// ==========================================
// File: src/components/stay/StayReviewSection.tsx
// ==========================================
import { useState, useMemo, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Star, X, CornerDownRight } from 'lucide-react';
import type { ReviewItem} from "../../api/reviews.ts";

interface StayReviewSectionProps {
    stayTitle: string;
    reviews: ReviewItem[];
}

export function StayReviewSection({ stayTitle, reviews }: StayReviewSectionProps) {
    const [isReviewModalOpen, setIsReviewModalOpen] = useState(false);

    const averageRating = useMemo(() => {
        if (reviews.length === 0) return '0.0';
        const sum = reviews.reduce((acc, cur) => acc + (cur.rating || 0), 0);
        return (sum / reviews.length).toFixed(1);
    }, [reviews]);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (isReviewModalOpen) {
            document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
        } else {
            document.body.style.overflow = 'unset';
        }
        return () => {
            document.body.style.overflow = 'unset';
        };
    }, [isReviewModalOpen]);

    return (
        <>
            {/* 1. 본문 리뷰 3개 미리보기 영역 */}
            <div style={{ marginTop: '22px', paddingTop: '20px', borderTop: '1px solid #e2e8f0' }}>
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: '12px' }}>
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px' }}>
                        <Star size={18} fill="#f59e0b" color="#f59e0b" />
                        <strong style={{ fontSize: '15px', color: '#0f172a' }}>
                            이용객 실제 리뷰 ({reviews.length}개)
                        </strong>
                        {reviews.length > 0 && (
                            <span style={{
                                fontSize: '13px',
                                fontWeight: 700,
                                color: '#0f766e',
                                backgroundColor: '#f0fdfa',
                                padding: '2px 8px',
                                borderRadius: '8px'
                            }}>
                                평점 ★ {averageRating}
                            </span>
                        )}
                    </div>

                    {reviews.length > 0 && (
                        <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => setIsReviewModalOpen(true)}
                            style={{
                                background: '#f8fafc',
                                border: '1px solid #cbd5e1',
                                borderRadius: '20px',
                                padding: '6px 14px',
                                fontSize: '12px',
                                fontWeight: 700,
                                color: '#123f4d',
                                cursor: 'pointer'
                            }}
                        >
                            전체 리뷰 보기 ({reviews.length})
                        </button>
                    )}
                </div>

                {reviews.length === 0 ? (
                    <div style={{
                        padding: '16px',
                        backgroundColor: '#f8fafc',
                        borderRadius: '10px',
                        textAlign: 'center',
                        fontSize: '13px',
                        color: '#94a3b8'
                    }}>
                        아직 작성된 리뷰가 없습니다. 숙소 이용 후기를 남겨 첫 주인공이 되어보세요!
                    </div>
                ) : (
                    <div style={{
                        display: 'grid',
                        gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fit, minmax(260px, 1fr))',
                        gap: '12px'
                    }}>
                        {reviews.slice(0, 3).map((rev) => (
                            <div
                                key={rev.reviewId}
                                onClick={() => setIsReviewModalOpen(true)}
                                style={{
                                    padding: '14px 16px',
                                    backgroundColor: '#f8fafc',
                                    borderRadius: '12px',
                                    border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                                    cursor: 'pointer',
                                    display: 'flex',
                                    flexDirection: 'column',
                                    justifyContent: 'space-between',
                                    gap: '8px'
                                }}
                            >
                                <div>
                                    <div style={{
                                        display: 'flex',
                                        justifyContent: 'space-between',
                                        alignItems: 'center',
                                        marginBottom: '6px'
                                    }}>
                                        <strong style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: '#0f172a' }}>{rev.authorName}</strong>
                                        <span style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: '#f59e0b', fontWeight: 700 }}>
                                            {'★'.repeat(rev.rating)}{'☆'.repeat(5 - rev.rating)}
                                        </span>
                                    </div>
                                    <p style={{
                                        margin: 0,
                                        fontSize: '13px',
                                        color: '#334155',
                                        lineHeight: 1.5,
                                        display: '-webkit-box',
                                        WebkitLineClamp: 2,
                                        WebkitBoxOrient: 'vertical',
                                        overflow: 'hidden'
                                    }}>
                                        {rev.content}
                                    </p>
                                </div>

                                <div style={{
                                    display: 'flex',
                                    justifyContent: 'space-between',
                                    alignItems: 'center',
                                    fontSize: '11px',
                                    color: '#94a3b8'
                                }}>
                                    <span>{rev.roomTitle}</span>
                                    {rev.hostReply && (
                                        <span style={{ color: '#0f766e', fontWeight: 700 }}>사장님 답글 있음</span>
                                    )}
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        ))}
                    </div>
                )}
            </div>

            {/* 2. 전체 리뷰 보기 팝업 모달 */}
            {isReviewModalOpen && (
                <div
                    style={{
                        position: 'fixed',
                        top: 0,
                        left: 0,
                        width: '100vw',
                        height: '100vh',
                        backgroundColor: 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.65)',
                        backdropFilter: 'blur(3px)',
                        zIndex: 3000,
                        display: 'flex',
                        alignItems: 'center',
                        justifyContent: 'center',
                        padding: '20px'
                    }}
                    onClick={() => setIsReviewModalOpen(false)}
                >
                    <div
                        style={{
                            width: '100%',
                            maxWidth: '720px',
                            maxHeight: '85vh',
                            backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                            borderRadius: '20px',
                            display: 'flex',
                            flexDirection: 'column',
                            overflow: 'hidden',
                            boxShadow: '0 25px 50px -12px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.3)'
                        }}
                        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
                    >
                        <div style={{ padding: '20px 24px', borderBottom: '1px solid #e2e8f0', display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                            <div>
                                <h3 style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: '18px', fontWeight: 800, color: '#0f172a' }}>
                                    {stayTitle} 전체 리뷰 ({reviews.length})
                                </h3>
                                <span style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: '#0f766e', fontWeight: 700 }}>
                                    평균 별점 ★ {averageRating} / 5.0
                                </span>
                            </div>
                            <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => setIsReviewModalOpen(false)}
                                style={{ background: 'none', border: 'none', cursor: 'pointer', color: '#64748b' }}
                            >
                                <X size={22} />
                            </button>
                        </div>

                        <div style={{ padding: '24px', overflowY: 'auto', display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '16px' }}>
                            {reviews.map((rev) => (
                                <div
                                    key={rev.reviewId}
                                    style={{
                                        padding: '18px',
                                        borderRadius: '14px',
                                        border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                                        backgroundColor: '#ffffff'
                                    }}
                                >
                                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: '6px' }}>
                                        <div>
                                            <strong style={{ fontSize: '14px', color: '#0f172a', marginRight: '8px' }}>{rev.authorName}</strong>
                                            <span style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: '#64748b' }}>· {rev.roomTitle}</span>
                                        </div>
                                        <span style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: '#94a3b8' }}>
                                            {rev.createdAt ? rev.createdAt.split('T')[0] : ''}
                                        </span>
                                    </div>

                                    <div style={{ color: '#f59e0b', fontSize: '14px', marginBottom: '8px' }}>
                                        {'★'.repeat(rev.rating)}{'☆'.repeat(5 - rev.rating)}
                                    </div>

                                    <p style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: '14px', color: '#334155', lineHeight: 1.6, whiteSpace: 'pre-line' }}>
                                        {rev.content}
                                    </p>

                                    {rev.hostReply && (
                                        <div style={{
                                            marginTop: '14px',
                                            padding: '14px 16px',
                                            backgroundColor: '#f0fdfa',
                                            borderRadius: '10px',
                                            border: '1px solid #ccfbf1'
                                        }}>
                                            <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '6px', marginBottom: '4px', fontSize: '12px', fontWeight: 700, color: '#0f766e' }}>
                                                <CornerDownRight size={14} /> {stayTitle}
                                                {rev.repliedAt && (
                                                    <span style={{ fontWeight: 400, color: '#64748b', marginLeft: '4px' }}>
                                                        ({rev.repliedAt.split('T')[0]})
                                                    </span>
                                                )}
                                            </div>
                                            <p style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: '13px', color: '#1e293b', lineHeight: 1.5, whiteSpace: 'pre-line' }}>
                                                {rev.hostReply}
                                            </p>
                                        </div>
                                    )}
                                </div>
                            ))}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}
        </>
    );
}
