import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import {
    Calendar,
    MapPin,
    Phone,
    Copy,
    BedDouble,
    Clock,
    XCircle,
    MessageSquarePlus,
    CheckCircle2
} from 'lucide-react';
import { money, getLodgingTypeName } from '../../data/stays.ts'
import type { MyReservationItem} from "../../pages/reservation/MyReservationsPage.tsx";

interface MyReservationCardProps {
    item: MyReservationItem;
    category: 'UPCOMING' | 'PAST' | 'CANCELED';
    todayStr: string;
    canceling: boolean;
    onCancel: (item: MyReservationItem) => void;
    onOpenReview: (item: MyReservationItem) => void;
}

export function MyReservationCard({
                                      item,
                                      category,
                                      todayStr,
                                      canceling,
                                      onCancel,
                                      onOpenReview
                                  }: MyReservationCardProps) {
    const navigate = useNavigate();

    const getNights = (checkIn: string, checkOut: string) => {
        const s = new Date(checkIn);
        const e = new Date(checkOut);
        const diff = Math.round((e.getTime() - s.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
        return diff > 0 ? diff : 1;
    };

    const getDDayBadge = (checkIn: string) => {
        const today = new Date(todayStr);
        const target = new Date(checkIn);
        const diff = Math.round((target.getTime() - today.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
        if (diff === 0) return '오늘 체크인';
        if (diff > 0) return `D-${diff}`;
        return '투숙 중';
    };

    const handleCopyAddress = (addr: string) => {
        navigator.clipboard.writeText(addr);
        alert('숙소 주소가 클립보드에 복사되었습니다.');
    };

    const nights = getNights(item.checkInDate, item.checkOutDate);
    const formattedDate = item.createdAt ? item.createdAt.split('T')[0].replace(/-/g, '.') : '';
    const orderNo = `#${item.checkInDate.replace(/-/g, '')}-${String(item.reservationId).padStart(4, '0')}`;

    return (
        <article
            style={{
                backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                borderRadius: '16px',
                border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                overflow: 'hidden',
                boxShadow: '0 2px 10px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.04)',
                opacity: category === 'CANCELED' ? 0.75 : 1
            }}
        >
            {/* 1. 상단 헤더: 예약번호 + 예약일시 + 상태 뱃지 */}
            <div style={{
                padding: '14px 22px',
                backgroundColor: '#f8fafc',
                borderBottom: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                display: 'flex',
                justifyContent: 'space-between',
                alignItems: 'center',
                flexWrap: 'wrap',
                gap: '8px'
            }}>
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '10px', fontSize: '13px' }}>
                    <strong style={{ color: '#0f172a', fontWeight: 700 }}>예약번호 {orderNo}</strong>
                    {formattedDate && (
                        <span style={{ color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: '12px' }}>
                            · {formattedDate} 결제
                        </span>
                    )}
                </div>

                <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px' }}>
                    {category === 'UPCOMING' && (
                        <span style={{
                            padding: '3px 10px',
                            borderRadius: '12px',
                            fontSize: '11px',
                            fontWeight: 700,
                            backgroundColor: '#123f4d',
                            color: '#ffffff'
                        }}>
                            {getDDayBadge(item.checkInDate)}
                        </span>
                    )}

                    <span style={{
                        padding: '4px 10px',
                        borderRadius: '12px',
                        fontSize: '12px',
                        fontWeight: 700,
                        backgroundColor:
                            category === 'UPCOMING'
                                ? '#ccfbf1'
                                : category === 'PAST'
                                    ? '#f1f5f9'
                                    : '#fee2e2',
                        color:
                            category === 'UPCOMING'
                                ? '#0f766e'
                                : category === 'PAST'
                                    ? '#475569'
                                    : '#dc2626'
                    }}>
                        {category === 'UPCOMING' && '예약 확정'}
                        {category === 'PAST' && '이용 완료'}
                        {category === 'CANCELED' && '예약 취소됨'}
                    </span>
                </div>
            </div>

            {/* 2. 중단 본문: 숙소 대표 이미지 + 숙소/객실 상세 정보 */}
            <div style={{
                padding: '20px 22px',
                display: 'grid',
                gridTemplateColumns: '160px 1fr',
                gap: '20px',
                alignItems: 'center'
            }}>
                <div
                    onClick={() => navigate(`/stays/${item.contentId}`)}
                    style={{
                        width: '160px',
                        height: '120px',
                        borderRadius: '12px',
                        backgroundImage: `url(${item.firstImage || '/jeju-stays.png'})`,
                        backgroundSize: 'cover',
                        backgroundPosition: 'center',
                        cursor: 'pointer',
                        border: '1px solid #e2e8f0'
                    }}
                />

                <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '6px' }}>
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '6px' }}>
                        <span style={{
                            fontSize: '11px',
                            fontWeight: 700,
                            color: '#0f766e',
                            backgroundColor: '#f0fdfa',
                            padding: '2px 8px',
                            borderRadius: '6px',
                            border: '1px solid #ccfbf1'
                        }}>
                            {getLodgingTypeName(item.lodgingType)}
                        </span>
                    </div>

                    <h3
                        onClick={() => navigate(`/stays/${item.contentId}`)}
                        style={{
                            fontSize: '18px',
                            fontWeight: 800,
                            color: '#0f172a',
                            margin: 0,
                            cursor: 'pointer',
                            display: 'inline-block'
                        }}
                    >
                        {item.stayTitle}
                    </h3>

                    <div style={{
                        display: 'flex',
                        alignItems: 'center',
                        gap: '6px',
                        fontSize: '14px',
                        fontWeight: 600,
                        color: '#334155',
                        marginTop: '2px'
                    }}>
                        <BedDouble size={15} color="#0f766e" />
                        <span>{item.roomTitle}</span>
                        <span style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: '#64748b', fontWeight: 400 }}>
                            (기준 {item.roomBaseCount || '2'}인 / 최대 {item.roomMaxCount || '4'}인)
                        </span>
                    </div>

                    <div style={{
                        display: 'flex',
                        alignItems: 'center',
                        flexWrap: 'wrap',
                        gap: '12px',
                        fontSize: '12px',
                        color: '#64748b',
                        marginTop: '4px'
                    }}>
                        {item.addr && (
                            <span style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '4px' }}>
                                <MapPin size={13} /> {item.addr}
                                <button
                                    type="button"
                                    onClick={() => handleCopyAddress(item.addr)}
                                    style={{
                                        marginLeft: '4px',
                                        padding: '2px 6px',
                                        fontSize: '11px',
                                        backgroundColor: '#f1f5f9',
                                        border: '1px solid #cbd5e1',
                                        borderRadius: '4px',
                                        color: '#475569',
                                        display: 'inline-flex',
                                        alignItems: 'center',
                                        gap: '3px',
                                        cursor: 'pointer'
                                    }}
                                >
                                    <Copy size={10} /> 복사
                                </button>
                            </span>
                        )}

                        {item.tel && (
                            <span style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '4px' }}>
                                <Phone size={13} /> {item.tel}
                            </span>
                        )}
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            {/* 3. 하단 요약 바: 일정 + 총 결제 금액 + 조건부 액션 버튼 */}
            <div style={{
                padding: '16px 22px',
                borderTop: '1px solid #f1f5f9',
                backgroundColor: '#fcfdfe',
                display: 'flex',
                justifyContent: 'space-between',
                alignItems: 'center',
                flexWrap: 'wrap',
                gap: '16px'
            }}>
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '16px', flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '6px', fontSize: '13px', color: '#1e293b' }}>
                        <Calendar size={15} color="#0f766e" />
                        <strong>{item.checkInDate}</strong>
                        <span style={{ color: '#94a3b8' }}>~</span>
                        <strong>{item.checkOutDate}</strong>
                        <span style={{
                            fontSize: '12px',
                            color: '#0f766e',
                            fontWeight: 700,
                            backgroundColor: '#f0fdfa',
                            padding: '2px 8px',
                            borderRadius: '10px'
                        }}>
                            {nights}박
                        </span>
                    </div>

                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '4px', fontSize: '12px', color: '#64748b' }}>
                        <Clock size={14} />
                        <span>
                            체크인 {item.checkinTime || '15:00'} / 체크아웃 {item.checkoutTime || '11:00'}
                        </span>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '16px', marginLeft: 'auto' }}>
                    <div style={{ textAlign: 'right' }}>
                        <span style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: '#94a3b8', display: 'block' }}>
                            총 결제 금액
                        </span>
                        <strong style={{
                            fontSize: '18px',
                            color: category === 'CANCELED' ? '#94a3b8' : '#0f766e',
                            textDecoration: category === 'CANCELED' ? 'line-through' : 'none'
                        }}>
                            {money(item.totalPrice)}
                        </strong>
                    </div>

                    {category === 'UPCOMING' && (
                        <button
                            type="button"
                            disabled={canceling}
                            onClick={() => onCancel(item)}
                            style={{
                                padding: '9px 16px',
                                backgroundColor: '#fff1f2',
                                color: '#e11d48',
                                border: '1px solid #fecdd3',
                                borderRadius: '8px',
                                fontSize: '13px',
                                fontWeight: 600,
                                cursor: canceling ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
                                display: 'flex',
                                alignItems: 'center',
                                gap: '5px'
                            }}
                        >
                            <XCircle size={15} />
                            {canceling ? '취소 처리 중...' : '예약 취소'}
                        </button>
                    )}

                    {category === 'PAST' && (
                        item.hasReview ? (
                            <button
                                type="button"
                                disabled
                                style={{
                                    padding: '9px 16px',
                                    backgroundColor: '#f1f5f9',
                                    color: '#94a3b8',
                                    border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                                    borderRadius: '8px',
                                    fontSize: '13px',
                                    fontWeight: 600,
                                    display: 'flex',
                                    alignItems: 'center',
                                    gap: '5px',
                                    cursor: 'default'
                                }}
                            >
                                <CheckCircle2 size={15} /> 리뷰 작성 완료
                            </button>
                        ) : (
                            <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => onOpenReview(item)}
                                style={{
                                    padding: '9px 16px',
                                    backgroundColor: '#123f4d',
                                    color: '#ffffff',
                                    border: 'none',
                                    borderRadius: '8px',
                                    fontSize: '13px',
                                    fontWeight: 600,
                                    cursor: 'pointer',
                                    display: 'flex',
                                    alignItems: 'center',
                                    gap: '5px'
                                }}
                            >
                                <MessageSquarePlus size={15} /> 리뷰 작성하기
                            </button>
                        )
                    )}
                </div>
            </div>
        </article>
    );
}


// ==========================================
// File: src/components/common/ChatWidget.tsx
// ==========================================
import React, { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import axios from 'axios';
import { MessageCircle, X, Send, Bot, User, RefreshCw } from 'lucide-react'; // 💡 RefreshCw(새로고침 아이콘) 추가

interface ChatMessage {
    sender: 'user' | 'ai';
    text: string;
}

export function ChatWidget() {
    const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);

    // 초기 환영 메시지를 상수로 분리
    const initialMessage: ChatMessage = {
        sender: 'ai',
        text: '안녕하세요! AI 야호 입니다. 제주도 여행에 대해 무엇이든 물어보세요! 🌴'
    };

    const [messages, setMessages] = useState<ChatMessage[]>([initialMessage]);
    const [inputValue, setInputValue] = useState('');
    const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
    const messagesEndRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

    const quickReplies = [
        "🌴 2박 3일 제주 커플 여행 코스 추천해줘",
        "💳 예약 취소 및 환불 규정이 어떻게 돼?",
        "🍊 제주도 흑돼지 맛집 알려줘"
    ];

    useEffect(() => {
        if (messagesEndRef.current) {
            messagesEndRef.current.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
        }
    }, [messages, isOpen]);

    // 💡 새 채팅(초기화) 기능 추가
    const handleResetChat = () => {
        if (window.confirm('대화 내용을 지우고 새로운 채팅을 시작하시겠습니까?')) {
            setMessages([initialMessage]);
            setInputValue('');
        }
    };

    const handleSendMessage = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (!inputValue.trim() || isLoading) return;

        const userText = inputValue.trim();
        setInputValue('');
        setMessages((prev) => [...prev, { sender: 'user', text: userText }]);
        setIsLoading(true);

        try {
            const res = await axios.post('/api/v1/chat', { message: userText });
            const aiText = res.data.response;
            setMessages((prev) => [...prev, { sender: 'ai', text: aiText }]);
        } catch (error) {
            setMessages((prev) => [...prev, { sender: 'ai', text: '죄송합니다. 현재 네트워크 오류로 답변을 드릴 수 없습니다.' }]);
        } finally {
            setIsLoading(false);
        }
    };

    return (
        <div style={{ position: 'fixed', bottom: '30px', right: '30px', zIndex: 9999 }}>
            {!isOpen && (
                <button
                    onClick={() => setIsOpen(true)}
                    style={{
                        width: '60px',
                        height: '60px',
                        borderRadius: '50%',
                        backgroundColor: '#123f4d',
                        color: '#ffffff',
                        border: 'none',
                        boxShadow: '0 4px 16px rgba(0,0,0,0.2)',
                        cursor: 'pointer',
                        display: 'flex',
                        alignItems: 'center',
                        justifyContent: 'center',
                        transition: 'transform 0.2s',
                    }}
                    onMouseEnter={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.transform = 'scale(1.08)')}
                    onMouseLeave={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.transform = 'scale(1)')}
                >
                    <MessageCircle size={28} />
                </button>
            )}

            {isOpen && (
                <div style={{
                    width: '350px',
                    height: '520px',
                    backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                    borderRadius: '16px',
                    boxShadow: '0 10px 40px rgba(0,0,0,0.2)',
                    display: 'flex',
                    flexDirection: 'column',
                    overflow: 'hidden',
                    border: '1px solid #e2e8f0'
                }}>
                    {/* 상단 헤더 */}
                    <div style={{
                        padding: '16px 20px',
                        backgroundColor: '#123f4d',
                        color: '#ffffff',
                        display: 'flex',
                        justifyContent: 'space-between',
                        alignItems: 'center'
                    }}>
                        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px' }}>
                            <Bot size={22} color="#6ee7b7" />
                            <strong style={{ fontSize: '15px' }}>제주 야호 AI 가이드</strong>
                        </div>

                        {/* 💡 초기화 버튼 & 닫기 버튼 배치 */}
                        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '14px' }}>
                            <button
                                onClick={handleResetChat}
                                title="새로운 채팅 시작"
                                style={{ background: 'none', border: 'none', color: '#ffffff', cursor: 'pointer', padding: 0, display: 'flex' }}
                            >
                                <RefreshCw size={17} />
                            </button>
                            <button
                                onClick={() => setIsOpen(false)}
                                title="창 닫기"
                                style={{ background: 'none', border: 'none', color: '#ffffff', cursor: 'pointer', padding: 0, display: 'flex' }}
                            >
                                <X size={20} />
                            </button>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* 대화 영역 */}
                    <div style={{
                        flex: 1,
                        padding: '20px',
                        overflowY: 'auto',
                        backgroundColor: '#f8fafc',
                        display: 'flex',
                        flexDirection: 'column',
                        gap: '16px'
                    }}>
                        {messages.map((msg, idx) => (
                            <div key={idx} style={{
                                display: 'flex',
                                gap: '10px',
                                alignItems: 'flex-start',
                                flexDirection: msg.sender === 'user' ? 'row-reverse' : 'row'
                            }}>
                                <div style={{
                                    width: '28px',
                                    height: '28px',
                                    borderRadius: '50%',
                                    backgroundColor: msg.sender === 'user' ? '#e2e8f0' : '#ccfbf1',
                                    display: 'flex',
                                    alignItems: 'center',
                                    justifyContent: 'center',
                                    flexShrink: 0
                                }}>
                                    {msg.sender === 'user' ? <User size={16} color="#64748b" /> : <Bot size={16} color="#0f766e" />}
                                </div>
                                <div style={{
                                    maxWidth: '75%',
                                    padding: '10px 14px',
                                    borderRadius: '14px',
                                    backgroundColor: msg.sender === 'user' ? '#123f4d' : '#ffffff',
                                    color: msg.sender === 'user' ? '#ffffff' : '#1e293b',
                                    border: msg.sender === 'user' ? 'none' : '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                                    fontSize: '13px',
                                    lineHeight: 1.5,
                                    borderTopLeftRadius: msg.sender === 'ai' ? '2px' : '14px',
                                    borderTopRightRadius: msg.sender === 'user' ? '2px' : '14px',
                                    whiteSpace: 'pre-line'
                                }}>
                                    {msg.text}
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        ))}
                        {isLoading && (
                            <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '10px', alignItems: 'flex-start' }}>
                                <div style={{ width: '28px', height: '28px', borderRadius: '50%', backgroundColor: '#ccfbf1', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}>
                                    <Bot size={16} color="#0f766e" />
                                </div>
                                <div style={{ padding: '10px 14px', borderRadius: '14px', backgroundColor: '#ffffff', border: '1px solid #e2e8f0', fontSize: '13px', color: '#94a3b8' }}>
                                    입력중...
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        )}
                        <div ref={messagesEndRef} />
                    </div>

                    {/* 빠른 질문(Quick Reply) 칩 영역 */}
                    {messages.length < 3 && (
                        <div style={{
                            display: 'flex',
                            flexDirection: 'column',
                            gap: '8px',
                            padding: '0 16px 12px 16px',
                            backgroundColor: '#f8fafc'
                        }}>
                            <span style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: '#94a3b8', fontWeight: 600 }}>이렇게 물어보세요!</span>
                            <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: '6px' }}>
                                {quickReplies.map((reply, idx) => (
                                    <button
                                        key={idx}
                                        onClick={() => setInputValue(reply)}
                                        style={{
                                            padding: '6px 12px',
                                            backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                                            border: '1px solid #cbd5e1',
                                            borderRadius: '16px',
                                            fontSize: '12px',
                                            color: '#123f4d',
                                            cursor: 'pointer',
                                            textAlign: 'left',
                                            boxShadow: '0 1px 2px rgba(0,0,0,0.02)'
                                        }}
                                    >
                                        {reply}
                                    </button>
                                ))}
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    )}

                    {/* 입력 영역 */}
                    <form onSubmit={handleSendMessage} style={{
                        padding: '12px',
                        borderTop: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                        backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                        display: 'flex',
                        gap: '8px'
                    }}>
                        <input
                            type="text"
                            value={inputValue}
                            onChange={(e) => setInputValue(e.target.value)}
                            placeholder="제주도 숙소에 대해 물어보세요..."
                            disabled={isLoading}
                            style={{
                                flex: 1,
                                padding: '10px 14px',
                                borderRadius: '20px',
                                border: '1px solid #cbd5e1',
                                fontSize: '13px',
                                outline: 'none'
                            }}
                        />
                        <button
                            type="submit"
                            disabled={!inputValue.trim() || isLoading}
                            style={{
                                width: '40px',
                                height: '40px',
                                borderRadius: '50%',
                                backgroundColor: inputValue.trim() && !isLoading ? '#123f4d' : '#e2e8f0',
                                color: '#ffffff',
                                border: 'none',
                                display: 'flex',
                                alignItems: 'center',
                                justifyContent: 'center',
                                cursor: inputValue.trim() && !isLoading ? 'pointer' : 'not-allowed'
                            }}
                        >
                            <Send size={16} style={{ marginLeft: '2px' }} />
                        </button>
                    </form>
                </div>
            )}
        </div>
    );
}

// File: src/components/common/DetailPhotoGallery.tsx
// ==========================================
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Image as ImageIcon, X } from 'lucide-react';

interface DetailPhotoGalleryProps {
    images: string[];
    emptyText?: string;
    modalTitle?: string;
    externalPreviewImage?: string | null; // 객실 사진 등 외부에서 클릭한 1장 확대용
    onCloseExternalPreview?: () => void;
}

export function DetailPhotoGallery({
                                       images,
                                       emptyText = '등록된 이미지가 없습니다.',
                                       modalTitle = '전체 사진',
                                       externalPreviewImage,
                                       onCloseExternalPreview
                                   }: DetailPhotoGalleryProps) {
    const [isGalleryOpen, setIsGalleryOpen] = useState(false);
    const [internalPreview, setInternalPreview] = useState<string | null>(null);

    const activePreview = externalPreviewImage || internalPreview;
    const mainImages = images.slice(0, 5);

    const closePreview = () => {
        setInternalPreview(null);
        if (onCloseExternalPreview) onCloseExternalPreview();
    };

    useEffect(() => {
        if (isGalleryOpen || Boolean(activePreview)) {
            document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
        } else {
            document.body.style.overflow = 'unset';
        }
        return () => {
            document.body.style.overflow = 'unset';
        };
    }, [isGalleryOpen, activePreview]);

    return (
        <>
            {/* 1. 상단 5분할 사진 배너 */}
            <section style={{ position: 'relative', marginBottom: '24px' }}>
                {mainImages.length > 0 ? (
                    <div
                        style={{
                            display: 'grid',
                            gridTemplateColumns: mainImages.length > 1 ? '1.2fr 1fr' : '1fr',
                            gap: '8px',
                            height: '420px',
                            borderRadius: '16px',
                            overflow: 'hidden',
                            backgroundColor: '#f1f5f9'
                        }}
                    >
                        <div
                            onClick={() => setInternalPreview(mainImages[0])}
                            style={{
                                backgroundImage: `url(${mainImages[0]})`,
                                backgroundSize: 'cover',
                                backgroundPosition: 'center',
                                height: '100%',
                                cursor: 'pointer',
                                transition: 'opacity 0.2s'
                            }}
                            onMouseEnter={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.opacity = '0.94')}
                            onMouseLeave={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.opacity = '1')}
                        />

                        {mainImages.length > 1 && (
                            <div
                                style={{
                                    display: 'grid',
                                    gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr',
                                    gridTemplateRows: '1fr 1fr',
                                    gap: '8px',
                                    height: '100%'
                                }}
                            >
                                {[1, 2, 3, 4].map((idx) => {
                                    const imgUrl = mainImages[idx];
                                    return (
                                        <div
                                            key={idx}
                                            onClick={() => imgUrl && setInternalPreview(imgUrl)}
                                            style={{
                                                backgroundImage: imgUrl ? `url(${imgUrl})` : 'none',
                                                backgroundColor: '#e2e8f0',
                                                backgroundSize: 'cover',
                                                backgroundPosition: 'center',
                                                height: '100%',
                                                cursor: imgUrl ? 'pointer' : 'default',
                                                transition: 'opacity 0.2s'
                                            }}
                                            onMouseEnter={(e) => {
                                                if (imgUrl) e.currentTarget.style.opacity = '0.94';
                                            }}
                                            onMouseLeave={(e) => {
                                                if (imgUrl) e.currentTarget.style.opacity = '1';
                                            }}
                                        />
                                    );
                                })}
                            </div>
                        )}
                    </div>
                ) : (
                    <div
                        style={{
                            height: '320px',
                            borderRadius: '16px',
                            backgroundColor: '#e2e8f0',
                            display: 'flex',
                            alignItems: 'center',
                            justifyContent: 'center',
                            color: '#94a3b8'
                        }}
                    >
                        {emptyText}
                    </div>
                )}

                {images.length > 0 && (
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setIsGalleryOpen(true)}
                        style={{
                            position: 'absolute',
                            right: '16px',
                            bottom: '16px',
                            backgroundColor: 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.95)',
                            color: '#0f172a',
                            border: '1px solid #cbd5e1',
                            borderRadius: '24px',
                            padding: '8px 16px',
                            fontSize: '13px',
                            fontWeight: 600,
                            display: 'flex',
                            alignItems: 'center',
                            gap: '6px',
                            cursor: 'pointer',
                            boxShadow: '0 4px 12px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.15)',
                            backdropFilter: 'blur(4px)'
                        }}
                    >
                        <ImageIcon size={16} />
                        전체 사진 ({images.length})
                    </button>
                )}
            </section>

            {/* 2. 전체 사진 모아보기 그리드 모달 */}
            {isGalleryOpen && (
                <div
                    style={{
                        position: 'fixed',
                        top: 0,
                        left: 0,
                        width: '100vw',
                        height: '100vh',
                        backgroundColor: 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.7)',
                        backdropFilter: 'blur(3px)',
                        zIndex: 2000,
                        display: 'flex',
                        alignItems: 'center',
                        justifyContent: 'center',
                        padding: '20px'
                    }}
                    onClick={() => setIsGalleryOpen(false)}
                >
                    <div
                        style={{
                            width: '100%',
                            maxWidth: '960px',
                            maxHeight: '90vh',
                            backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                            borderRadius: '16px',
                            display: 'flex',
                            flexDirection: 'column',
                            overflow: 'hidden',
                            boxShadow: '0 25px 50px -12px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.25)'
                        }}
                        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
                    >
                        <div
                            style={{
                                display: 'flex',
                                justifyContent: 'space-between',
                                alignItems: 'center',
                                padding: '18px 24px',
                                borderBottom: '1px solid #e2e8f0'
                            }}
                        >
                            <div>
                                <h3 style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: '18px', color: '#0f172a', fontWeight: 700 }}>
                                    {modalTitle} <span style={{ color: '#0f766e', fontSize: '15px' }}>({images.length})</span>
                                </h3>
                                <span style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: '#64748b' }}>
                                    사진을 클릭하면 크게 볼 수 있습니다.
                                </span>
                            </div>
                            <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => setIsGalleryOpen(false)}
                                style={{ background: 'none', border: 'none', cursor: 'pointer', color: '#64748b' }}
                            >
                                <X size={22} />
                            </button>
                        </div>

                        <div
                            style={{
                                padding: '24px',
                                overflowY: 'auto',
                                display: 'grid',
                                gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fill, minmax(260px, 1fr))',
                                gap: '14px'
                            }}
                        >
                            {images.map((url, idx) => (
                                <div
                                    key={idx}
                                    onClick={() => setInternalPreview(url)}
                                    style={{
                                        width: '100%',
                                        height: '190px',
                                        borderRadius: '10px',
                                        overflow: 'hidden',
                                        backgroundColor: '#f1f5f9',
                                        border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                                        cursor: 'zoom-in',
                                        transition: 'transform 0.15s ease'
                                    }}
                                    onMouseEnter={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.transform = 'scale(1.02)')}
                                    onMouseLeave={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.transform = 'scale(1.0)')}
                                >
                                    <img
                                        src={url}
                                        alt={`gallery-${idx}`}
                                        style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%', objectFit: 'cover', display: 'block' }}
                                        loading="lazy"
                                    />
                                </div>
                            ))}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}

            {/* 3. 클릭한 사진 1장 중앙 확대 팝업 (Lightbox) */}
            {activePreview && (
                <div
                    style={{
                        position: 'fixed',
                        top: 0,
                        left: 0,
                        width: '100vw',
                        height: '100vh',
                        backgroundColor: 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.86)',
                        backdropFilter: 'blur(5px)',
                        zIndex: 4000,
                        display: 'flex',
                        alignItems: 'center',
                        justifyContent: 'center',
                        padding: '24px',
                        cursor: 'zoom-out'
                    }}
                    onClick={closePreview}
                >
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={closePreview}
                        aria-label="사진 닫기"
                        style={{
                            position: 'absolute',
                            top: '24px',
                            right: '24px',
                            width: '44px',
                            height: '44px',
                            borderRadius: '50%',
                            border: '1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.3)',
                            backgroundColor: 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.7)',
                            color: '#ffffff',
                            display: 'flex',
                            alignItems: 'center',
                            justifyContent: 'center',
                            cursor: 'pointer'
                        }}
                    >
                        <X size={24} />
                    </button>

                    <img
                        src={activePreview}
                        alt="확대 사진"
                        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
                        style={{
                            maxWidth: '90vw',
                            maxHeight: '88vh',
                            objectFit: 'contain',
                            borderRadius: '14px',
                            boxShadow: '0 25px 50px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5)',
                            backgroundColor: '#0f172a',
                            cursor: 'default'
                        }}
                    />
                </div>
            )}
        </>
    );
}


// ==========================================
// File: src/components/common/DateRangePicker.tsx
// ==========================================
import { useState, useRef, useEffect, useMemo } from 'react';
import { Calendar, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight } from 'lucide-react';

interface DateRangePickerProps {
    checkIn: string;   // 'YYYY-MM-DD'
    checkOut: string;  // 'YYYY-MM-DD'
    onChange: (checkIn: string, checkOut: string) => void;
}

export function DateRangePicker({ checkIn, checkOut, onChange }: DateRangePickerProps) {
    const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
    const [selectingStart, setSelectingStart] = useState<string | null>(null);
    const [hoverDate, setHoverDate] = useState<string | null>(null);

    // 달력 기준월 (체크인 날짜 기준 또는 오늘 기준)
    const [viewDate, setViewDate] = useState(() => {
        const d = checkIn ? new Date(checkIn) : new Date();
        return new Date(d.getFullYear(), d.getMonth(), 1);
    });


    const pickerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

    // 오늘 날짜 문자열
    const todayStr = useMemo(() => new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0], []);

    // 외부 클릭 시 닫기
    useEffect(() => {
        const handleClickOutside = (e: MouseEvent) => {
            if (pickerRef.current && !pickerRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) {
                setIsOpen(false);
                setSelectingStart(null);
            }
        };
        if (isOpen) document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
        return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
    }, [isOpen]);

    // 이전달 / 다음달 이동
    const handlePrevMonth = () => {
        setViewDate(new Date(viewDate.getFullYear(), viewDate.getMonth() - 1, 1));
    };
    const handleNextMonth = () => {
        setViewDate(new Date(viewDate.getFullYear(), viewDate.getMonth() + 1, 1));
    };

    // 날짜 클릭 핸들러
    const handleDateClick = (dateStr: string) => {
        if (!selectingStart) {
            // 첫 번째 클릭: 체크인 설정
            setSelectingStart(dateStr);
        } else {
            // 두 번째 클릭: 체크아웃 설정
            if (dateStr > selectingStart) {
                onChange(selectingStart, dateStr);
                setSelectingStart(null);
                setIsOpen(false);
            } else {
                // 이전 날짜를 클릭한 경우 다시 체크인으로 세팅
                setSelectingStart(dateStr);
            }
        }
    };

    // 박수 계산
    const nights = useMemo(() => {
        if (!checkIn || !checkOut) return 0;
        const s = new Date(checkIn);
        const e = new Date(checkOut);
        const diff = (e.getTime() - s.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24);
        return diff > 0 ? diff : 0;
    }, [checkIn, checkOut]);

    // 특정 월의 날짜 그리드 생성 헬퍼
    const renderCalendarMonth = (year: number, month: number) => {
        const firstDayOfWeek = new Date(year, month, 1).getDay(); // 0(일) ~ 6(토)
        const daysInMonth = new Date(year, month + 1, 0).getDate();

        const activeStart = selectingStart || checkIn;
        const activeEnd = selectingStart ? (hoverDate || selectingStart) : checkOut;

        const cells = [];
        for (let i = 0; i < firstDayOfWeek; i++) {
            cells.push(<div key={`empty-${i}`} style={{ height: '36px' }} />);
        }

        for (let d = 1; d <= daysInMonth; d++) {
            const dateStr = `${year}-${String(month + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(d).padStart(2, '0')}`;
            const isPast = dateStr < todayStr;
            const isStart = dateStr === activeStart;
            const isEnd = dateStr === activeEnd;
            const isInRange = activeStart && activeEnd && dateStr > activeStart && dateStr < activeEnd;

            cells.push(
                <button
                    key={dateStr}
                    type="button"
                    disabled={isPast}
                    onClick={() => handleDateClick(dateStr)}
                    onMouseEnter={() => selectingStart && setHoverDate(dateStr)}
                    style={{
                        height: '36px',
                        width: '100%',
                        border: 'none',
                        background: isStart || isEnd ? '#123f4d' : isInRange ? '#e6f4f1' : 'transparent',
                        color: isStart || isEnd ? '#ffffff' : isPast ? '#cbd5e1' : '#1e293b',
                        fontWeight: isStart || isEnd ? 700 : 500,
                        fontSize: '13px',
                        cursor: isPast ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
                        borderRadius: isStart ? '8px 0 0 8px' : isEnd ? '0 8px 8px 0' : '0',
                        display: 'flex',
                        alignItems: 'center',
                        justifyContent: 'center',
                        transition: 'background 0.15s ease'
                    }}
                >
                    {d}
                </button>
            );
        }

        return (
            <div style={{ width: '260px' }}>
                <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', fontWeight: 700, fontSize: '15px', marginBottom: '14px', color: '#0f172a' }}>
                    {year}년 {month + 1}월
                </div>
                <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(7, 1fr)', gap: '2px', textAlign: 'center', fontSize: '12px', color: '#94a3b8', marginBottom: '8px' }}>
                    <span>일</span><span>월</span><span>화</span><span>수</span><span>목</span><span>금</span><span>토</span>
                </div>
                <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(7, 1fr)', gap: '2px 0' }}>
                    {cells}
                </div>
            </div>
        );
    };

    return (
        <div ref={pickerRef} style={{ position: 'relative', width: '100%' }}>
            {/* 1. 트리거 인풋 버튼 */}
            <div
                onClick={() => setIsOpen((prev) => !prev)}
                style={{
                    display: 'flex',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                    gap: '10px',
                    cursor: 'pointer',
                    userSelect: 'none'
                }}
            >
                <Calendar size={20} color="#49717a" style={{ flexShrink: 0 }} />
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column' }}>
                    <span style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: '#819296', fontWeight: 600 }}>
                        일정 {nights > 0 && `(${nights}박)`}
                    </span>
                    <strong style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: '#17343d', marginTop: '2px' }}>
                        {checkIn ? checkIn.slice(5) : '체크인'} ~ {checkOut ? checkOut.slice(5) : '체크아웃'}
                    </strong>
                </div>
            </div>

            {/* 2. 팝업 모달창 (2개월 듀얼 뷰) */}
            {isOpen && (
                <div
                    style={{
                        position: 'absolute',
                        top: 'calc(100% + 14px)',
                        left: 0,
                        backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                        borderRadius: '16px',
                        boxShadow: '0 12px 36px rgba(0,0,0,0.18)',
                        border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                        padding: '24px 28px',
                        zIndex: 1000,
                        display: 'flex',
                        flexDirection: 'column',
                        gap: '20px'
                    }}
                >
                    {/* 상단 월 이동 네비게이션 */}
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                        <span style={{ fontSize: '14px', fontWeight: 600, color: '#0f766e' }}>
                            {selectingStart ? '체크아웃 날짜를 선택해 주세요' : '체크인 날짜를 선택해 주세요'}
                        </span>
                        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '6px' }}>
                            <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={handlePrevMonth}
                                style={{ background: '#f1f5f9', border: 'none', borderRadius: '50%', width: '32px', height: '32px', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', cursor: 'pointer' }}
                            >
                                <ChevronLeft size={16} color="#475569" />
                            </button>
                            <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={handleNextMonth}
                                style={{ background: '#f1f5f9', border: 'none', borderRadius: '50%', width: '32px', height: '32px', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', cursor: 'pointer' }}
                            >
                                <ChevronRight size={16} color="#475569" />
                            </button>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* 달력 본체: 현재달 & 다음달 듀얼 렌더링 */}
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '32px' }}>
                        {renderCalendarMonth(viewDate.getFullYear(), viewDate.getMonth())}
                        {renderCalendarMonth(
                            viewDate.getMonth() === 11 ? viewDate.getFullYear() + 1 : viewDate.getFullYear(),
                            (viewDate.getMonth() + 1) % 12
                        )}
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}
        </div>
    );
}


// ==========================================
// File: src/components/common/PageHeader.tsx
// ==========================================
import React from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { ChevronLeft } from 'lucide-react';

interface PageHeaderProps {
    title: string;
    onBack?: () => void;
    rightElement?: React.ReactNode; // 우측 'HOST 파트너 센터' 뱃지나 셀렉트 박스 등
    titleSize?: string;
}

export function PageHeader({
                               title,
                               onBack,
                               rightElement,
                               titleSize = '18px'
                           }: PageHeaderProps) {
    const navigate = useNavigate();

    return (
        <header
            style={{
                backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                borderBottom: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                position: 'sticky',
                top: 0,
                zIndex: 100
            }}
        >
            <div
                className="container"
                style={{
                    height: '60px',
                    display: 'flex',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                    justifyContent: 'space-between',
                    gap: '16px'
                }}
            >
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '16px' }}>
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => (onBack ? onBack() : navigate(-1))}
                        style={{
                            background: 'none',
                            border: 'none',
                            cursor: 'pointer',
                            display: 'flex',
                            alignItems: 'center'
                        }}
                        aria-label="뒤로 가기"
                    >
                        <ChevronLeft size={24} color="#123f4d" />
                    </button>
                    <span style={{ fontSize: titleSize, fontWeight: 700, color: '#123f4d' }}>
                        {title}
                    </span>
                </div>

                {rightElement && <div>{rightElement}</div>}
            </div>
        </header>
    );
}


// ==========================================
// File: src/components/common/NotificationBell.tsx
// ==========================================
import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { Bell, X } from 'lucide-react';

interface NotificationItem {
    id: string;
    title: string;
    message: string;
    type: string;
    createdAt: string;
}

interface NotificationBellProps {
    userRole: string;
    isLoggedIn: boolean;
}

export function NotificationBell({ userRole, isLoggedIn }: NotificationBellProps) {
    const [notifications, setNotifications] = useState<NotificationItem[]>([]);
    const [isNotiOpen, setIsNotiOpen] = useState(false);
    const notiRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

    // 알림 목록 조회 (ROLE_HOST 및 ROLE_ADMIN 전용)
    useEffect(() => {
        if (isLoggedIn && (userRole === 'ROLE_HOST' || userRole === 'ROLE_ADMIN')) {
            const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
            fetch('/api/v1/notifications', {
                headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }
            })
                .then((res) => (res.ok ? res.json() : []))
                .then((data: NotificationItem[]) => {
                    const dismissed: string[] = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('dismissedNotiIds') || '[]');
                    const filtered = data.filter((item) => !dismissed.includes(item.id));
                    setNotifications(filtered);
                })
                .catch(() => setNotifications([]));
        }
    }, [isLoggedIn, userRole]);

    // 알림창 바깥 클릭 시 닫기
    useEffect(() => {
        const handleClickOutsideNoti = (event: MouseEvent) => {
            if (notiRef.current && !notiRef.current.contains(event.target as Node)) {
                setIsNotiOpen(false);
            }
        };
        document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutsideNoti);
        return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutsideNoti);
    }, []);

    // X 버튼 클릭 시 로컬 삭제 처리
    const handleDeleteNoti = (id: string) => {
        const dismissed: string[] = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('dismissedNotiIds') || '[]');
        if (!dismissed.includes(id)) {
            dismissed.push(id);
            localStorage.setItem('dismissedNotiIds', JSON.stringify(dismissed));
        }
        setNotifications((prev) => prev.filter((item) => item.id !== id));
    };

    // 호스트나 관리자가 아니면 아무것도 렌더링하지 않음
    if (!isLoggedIn || (userRole !== 'ROLE_HOST' && userRole !== 'ROLE_ADMIN')) {
        return null;
    }

    return (
        <div style={{ position: 'relative' }} ref={notiRef}>
            <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setIsNotiOpen((prev) => !prev)}
                style={{
                    position: 'relative',
                    width: '38px',
                    height: '38px',
                    borderRadius: '50%',
                    backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                    border: '1.5px solid #123f4d',
                    display: 'flex',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                    justifyContent: 'center',
                    cursor: 'pointer',
                    color: '#123f4d'
                }}
                aria-label="알림"
            >
                <Bell size={18} />

                {notifications.length > 0 && (
                    <span
                        style={{
                            position: 'absolute',
                            top: '3px',
                            left: '3px',
                            width: '8px',
                            height: '8px',
                            backgroundColor: '#e53e3e',
                            borderRadius: '50%',
                            border: '1.5px solid #ffffff'
                        }}
                    />
                )}
            </button>

            {isNotiOpen && (
                <div
                    style={{
                        position: 'absolute',
                        top: '115%',
                        right: 0,
                        width: '300px',
                        backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                        borderRadius: '12px',
                        boxShadow: '0 8px 24px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.15)',
                        border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                        padding: '12px',
                        zIndex: 1100
                    }}
                >
                    <div
                        style={{
                            display: 'flex',
                            justifyContent: 'space-between',
                            alignItems: 'center',
                            marginBottom: '8px',
                            paddingBottom: '6px',
                            borderBottom: '1px solid #f1f5f9'
                        }}
                    >
                        <strong style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: '#1e293b' }}>알림 내역</strong>
                        <span style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: '#64748b' }}>{notifications.length}건</span>
                    </div>

                    {notifications.length === 0 ? (
                        <div style={{ padding: '20px 0', textAlign: 'center', color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: '12px' }}>
                            새로운 알림이 없습니다.
                        </div>
                    ) : (
                        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '8px', maxHeight: '250px', overflowY: 'auto' }}>
                            {notifications.map((item) => (
                                <div
                                    key={item.id}
                                    style={{
                                        display: 'flex',
                                        justifyContent: 'space-between',
                                        alignItems: 'flex-start',
                                        backgroundColor: '#f8fafc',
                                        padding: '8px 10px',
                                        borderRadius: '8px',
                                        border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                                        gap: '8px'
                                    }}
                                >
                                    <div>
                                        <p
                                            style={{
                                                margin: '0 0 3px 0',
                                                fontSize: '12px',
                                                fontWeight: 600,
                                                color: item.type === 'ACCOM_REJECT' ? '#e53e3e' : '#0f766e'
                                            }}
                                        >
                                            {item.title}
                                        </p>
                                        <p style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: '11px', color: '#475569', wordBreak: 'break-word', lineHeight: '1.4' }}>
                                            {item.message}
                                        </p>
                                    </div>
                                    <button
                                        type="button"
                                        onClick={() => handleDeleteNoti(item.id)}
                                        style={{
                                            background: 'none',
                                            border: 'none',
                                            cursor: 'pointer',
                                            padding: '2px',
                                            color: '#94a3b8'
                                        }}
                                        aria-label="알림 지우기"
                                    >
                                        <X size={14} />
                                    </button>
                                </div>
                            ))}
                        </div>
                    )}
                </div>
            )}
        </div>
    );
}
