import axios from 'axios';
import type { Stay } from '../types/stay';

// 1. 지역 필터
export const regions = [
 '전체 지역',
 '제주시',
 '애월·한림',
 '함덕·구좌',
 '서귀포·중문',
 '성산·표선',
] as const;

// 2. 숙박 유형 필터
export const types = [
 '전체 숙박',
 '호텔',
 '콘도/리조트',
 '펜션/풀빌라',
 '모텔',
 '캠핑/글램핑',
 '게스트하우스/호스텔',
] as const;

// 3. 메인 추천 여행지 배너
export const destinations = [
 { name: '애월·한림', note: '해안도로와 석양', className: 'spot-a' },
 { name: '서귀포·중문', note: '폭포와 남쪽 바다', className: 'spot-b' },
 { name: '성산·표선', note: '일출과 오름', className: 'spot-c' },
];

// 4. 금액 포맷팅 헬퍼
export const money = (n?: number) => {
 if (!n || n <= 0) return '요금 문의';
 return new Intl.NumberFormat('ko-KR').format(n) + '원';
};

// 5. DB 코드(AC01~AC06)를 한글 숙박 유형명으로 변환
export const getLodgingTypeName = (code?: string): string => {
 switch (code) {
  case 'AC01': return '호텔';
  case 'AC02': return '콘도미니엄';
  case 'AC03': return '펜션/민박';
  case 'AC04': return '모텔';
  case 'AC05': return '캠핑';
  case 'AC06': return '호스텔';
  default: return code || '숙소';
 }
};

// 6. 지역 필터명을 백엔드 검색 키워드로 매핑
export const mapRegionToKeyword = (region: string): string => {
 if (region === '전체 지역' || !region) return '';
 if (region === '애월·한림') return '애월';
 if (region === '함덕·구좌') return '구좌';
 if (region === '서귀포·중문') return '서귀포';
 if (region === '성산·표선') return '성산';
 return region; // '제주시' 등
};

// 7. 숙박 유형명을 백엔드 파라미터(AC01~AC06)로 매핑
export const mapTypeToKeyword = (type: string): string => {
 if (!type || type === '전체 숙박') return '';
 if (type === '호텔') return 'AC01';
 if (type.includes('콘도') || type.includes('리조트')) return 'AC02';
 if (type.includes('펜션')) return 'AC03';
 if (type.includes('모텔')) return 'AC04';
 if (type.includes('캠핑')) return 'AC05';
 if (type.includes('호스텔') || type.includes('게스트하우스')) return 'AC06';
 return type;
};

// 8. 백엔드 숙소 목록 비동기 조회 함수
export const fetchStays = async (
    region?: string,
    type?: string,
    query?: string,
    checkIn?: string,
    checkOut?: string,
    guests?: number // 💡 추가
): Promise<Stay[]> => {
 const params: Record<string, string | number> = {};

 const regionKeyword = mapRegionToKeyword(region || '');
 if (regionKeyword) params.region = regionKeyword;

 const typeKeyword = mapTypeToKeyword(type || '');
 if (typeKeyword) params.type = typeKeyword;

 if (query && query.trim()) params.query = query.trim();
 if (checkIn && checkIn.trim()) params.checkIn = checkIn.trim();
 if (checkOut && checkOut.trim()) params.checkOut = checkOut.trim();
 if (guests && guests > 0) params.guests = guests; // 💡 추가

 const response = await axios.get<Stay[]>('/api/v1/stays', { params });
 return response.data;
};

    overview: string;
    firstImage: string;
    firstImage2: string;
    mapX: string;
    mapY: string;
    hostName: string;
    businessNo?: string;
    rooms: RoomDto[];
}

export function StayDetailPage() {
    const { contentId } = useParams<{ contentId: string }>();
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const location = useLocation();

    const todayStr = useMemo(() => new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0], []);
    const tomorrowStr = useMemo(() => {
        const d = new Date();
        d.setDate(d.getDate() + 1);
        return d.toISOString().split('T')[0];
    }, []);

    const incomingDates = (location.state as { checkIn?: string; checkOut?: string }) || {};
    const [checkIn, setCheckIn] = useState(incomingDates.checkIn || todayStr);
    const [checkOut, setCheckOut] = useState(incomingDates.checkOut || tomorrowStr);

    const [stay, setStay] = useState<StayDetail | null>(null);
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
    const [roomPreviewImage, setRoomPreviewImage] = useState<string | null>(null);
    const [isSellerInfoOpen, setIsSellerInfoOpen] = useState(false);
    const [isLiked, setIsLiked] = useState(false);
    const [isMapPopupOpen, setIsMapPopupOpen] = useState(false);

    const [nearbyTours, setNearbyTours] = useState<NearbyTourItem[]>([]);
    const [reviews, setReviews] = useState<ReviewItem[]>([]);

    useEffect(() => {
        window.scrollTo(0, 0);
    }, [contentId]);

    // 숙소 찜 상태 확인
    useEffect(() => {
        const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
        if (token && contentId) {
            fetch(`/api/v1/likes/accommodations/${contentId}/status`, {
                headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }
            })
                .then((res) => (res.ok ? res.json() : false))
                .then((data) => setIsLiked(data))
                .catch(() => setIsLiked(false));
        }
    }, [contentId]);

    // 숙소 상세 및 날짜별 잔여 객실 데이터 조회
    useEffect(() => {
        if (!contentId) return;
        axios.get<StayDetail>(`/api/v1/stays/${contentId}`, {
            params: {
                checkIn: checkIn || undefined,
                checkOut: checkOut || undefined,
            }
        })
            .then((res) => setStay(res.data))
            .catch((err) => {
                console.error(err);
                alert('숙소 정보를 불러오지 못했습니다.');
                navigate('/');
            })
            .finally(() => setLoading(false));
    }, [contentId, checkIn, checkOut, navigate]);

    // 주변 20km 이내 관광지 최대 9개 조회
    useEffect(() => {
        if (!contentId) return;
        fetchNearbyTours(contentId)
            .then((data) => setNearbyTours(data.slice(0, 9)))
            .catch(() => setNearbyTours([]));
    }, [contentId]);

    // 숙소 리뷰 목록 조회
    useEffect(() => {
        if (!contentId) return;
        fetchStayReviews(contentId)
            .then((data) => setReviews(data || []))
            .catch(() => setReviews([]));
    }, [contentId]);

    const handleToggleLike = async () => {
        const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
        if (!token) {
            alert('로그인이 필요한 기능입니다.');
            navigate('/login');
            return;
        }
        if (!contentId) return;

        try {
            const res = await toggleAccommodationLike(contentId);
            setIsLiked(res.liked);
            alert(res.message);
        } catch {
            alert('오류가 발생했습니다.');
        }
    };

    const allGalleryImages = useMemo(() => {
        if (!stay) return [];
        const set = new Set<string>();
        if (stay.firstImage) set.add(stay.firstImage);
        if (stay.firstImage2) set.add(stay.firstImage2);
        stay.rooms?.forEach((r) => {
            r.imageUrls?.forEach((url) => {
                if (url) set.add(url);
            });
        });
        return Array.from(set);
    }, [stay]);

    if (loading) {
        return (
            <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', padding: '120px 0', color: '#64748b' }}>
                숙소 상세 정보를 불러오는 중입니다...
            </div>
        );
    }

    if (!stay) return null;

    const facilities = stay.subFacility ? stay.subFacility.split(',').map((f) => f.trim()) : [];

    return (
        <div style={{ backgroundColor: '#f8fafc', minHeight: '100vh' }}>
            {/* 1. 공통 상단 헤더 */}
            <PageHeader title={stay.title} titleSize="22px" />

            <main className="container" style={{ maxWidth: '1050px', margin: '24px auto', padding: '0 16px' }}>
                {/* 2. 공통 사진 갤러리 (5분할 배너 + 전체 사진 모달 + 1장 확대 라이트박스) */}
                <DetailPhotoGallery
                    images={allGalleryImages}
                    emptyText="등록된 이미지가 없습니다."
                    modalTitle="전체 사진"
                    externalPreviewImage={roomPreviewImage}
                    onCloseExternalPreview={() => setRoomPreviewImage(null)}
                />

                {/* 3. 숙소 기본 정보 및 리뷰 미리보기 섹션 */}
                <section style={{
                    backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                    borderRadius: '16px',
                    padding: '28px',
                    border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                    marginBottom: '24px',
                    position: 'relative'
                }}>
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={handleToggleLike}
                        style={{
                            position: 'absolute',
                            top: '24px',
                            right: '28px',
                            background: 'none',
                            border: 'none',
                            cursor: 'pointer',
                            padding: '6px',
                            display: 'flex',
                            alignItems: 'center',
                            justifyContent: 'center',
                            transition: 'transform 0.15s ease',
                        }}
                        onMouseEnter={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.transform = 'scale(1.1)')}
                        onMouseLeave={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.transform = 'scale(1.0)')}
                        aria-label="숙소 찜하기"
                    >
                        <Heart
                            size={40}
                            fill={isLiked ? '#ef4444' : 'none'}
                            color={isLiked ? '#ef4444' : '#94a3b8'}
                            strokeWidth={1.7}
                        />
                    </button>

                    <span style={{
                        fontSize: '13px',
                        fontWeight: 600,
                        color: '#0f766e',
                        backgroundColor: '#ccfbf1',
                        padding: '3px 8px',
                        borderRadius: '6px'
                    }}>
                        {getLodgingTypeName(stay.lodgingType)}
                    </span>
                    <h1 style={{ fontSize: '26px', color: '#0f172a', margin: '12px 0 8px 0', paddingRight: '60px' }}>
                        {stay.title}
                    </h1>
                    <p style={{
                        display: 'flex',
                        alignItems: 'center',
                        gap: '6px',
                        color: '#64748b',
                        fontSize: '14px',
                        margin: '0 0 16px 0'
                    }}>
                        <MapPin size={17} /> {stay.addr} {stay.zipcode && `(${stay.zipcode})`}
                    </p>

                    <div style={{
                        display: 'grid',
                        gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fit, minmax(200px, 1fr))',
                        gap: '12px',
                        padding: '16px',
                        backgroundColor: '#f1f5f9',
                        borderRadius: '12px',
                        fontSize: '13px',
                        color: '#334155'
                    }}>
                        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px' }}>
                            <Clock size={16} color="#0f766e" />
                            <span>체크인 {stay.checkinTime || '15:00'} ~ 체크아웃 {stay.checkoutTime || '11:00'}</span>
                        </div>
                        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px' }}>
                            <Phone size={16} color="#0f766e" />
                            <span>{stay.tel || '안내 번호 없음'}</span>
                        </div>
                        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px' }}>
                            <Car size={16} color="#0f766e" />
                            <span>주차: {stay.parking || '주차 가능 여부 문의'}</span>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* 분리된 리뷰 미리보기 및 전체 리뷰 모달 컴포넌트 */}
                    <StayReviewSection stayTitle={stay.title} reviews={reviews} />
                </section>

                {/* 4. 숙소 소개 및 찾아오시는 길 */}
                <section style={{
                    backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                    borderRadius: '16px',
                    padding: '28px',
                    border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                    marginBottom: '24px'
                }}>
                    <h2 style={{ fontSize: '18px', color: '#0f172a', marginBottom: '14px' }}>숙소 소개</h2>
                    <p style={{
                        color: '#475569',
                        fontSize: '14px',
                        lineHeight: '1.7',
                        whiteSpace: 'pre-line',
                        margin: '0 0 24px 0'
                    }}>
                        {stay.overview || '등록된 숙소 상세 소개글이 없습니다.'}
                    </p>

                    {facilities.length > 0 && (
                        <>
                            <h3 style={{ fontSize: '15px', color: '#0f172a', marginBottom: '12px' }}>편의시설 및 서비스</h3>
                            <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: '8px' }}>
                                {facilities.map((fac, idx) => (
                                    <span
                                        key={idx}
                                        style={{
                                            display: 'flex',
                                            alignItems: 'center',
                                            gap: '5px',
                                            padding: '6px 12px',
                                            backgroundColor: '#f8fafc',
                                            border: '1px solid #cbd5e1',
                                            borderRadius: '20px',
                                            fontSize: '12px',
                                            color: '#334155'
                                        }}
                                    >
                                        <CheckCircle2 size={13} color="#0f766e" /> {fac}
                                    </span>
                                ))}
                            </div>
                        </>
                    )}

                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', margin: '24px 0 12px 0' }}>
                        <h3 style={{ fontSize: '15px', color: '#0f172a', margin: 0 }}>찾아오시는 길</h3>
                        {stay.mapX && stay.mapY && (
                            <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => setIsMapPopupOpen(true)}
                                style={{
                                    display: 'inline-flex',
                                    alignItems: 'center',
                                    gap: '5px',
                                    padding: '6px 12px',
                                    borderRadius: '20px',
                                    border: '1px solid #cbd5e1',
                                    backgroundColor: '#f8fafc',
                                    color: '#123f4d',
                                    fontSize: '12px',
                                    fontWeight: 700,
                                    cursor: 'pointer'
                                }}
                            >
                                <Maximize2 size={13} /> 지도 크게 보기
                            </button>
                        )}
                    </div>

                    {stay.mapX && stay.mapY && (
                        <div
                            onClick={() => setIsMapPopupOpen(true)}
                            style={{
                                position: 'relative',
                                borderRadius: '12px',
                                overflow: 'hidden',
                                border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                                cursor: 'pointer'
                            }}
                        >
                            <KakaoStayMap
                                stays={[{ ...stay, basePrice: 0 } as any]}
                                mode="detail"
                                level={4}
                                height="360px"
                                zoomable={false}
                            />
                            <div
                                style={{
                                    position: 'absolute',
                                    top: '12px',
                                    left: '12px',
                                    backgroundColor: 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.78)',
                                    backdropFilter: 'blur(4px)',
                                    color: '#ffffff',
                                    padding: '6px 12px',
                                    borderRadius: '20px',
                                    fontSize: '12px',
                                    fontWeight: 600,
                                    display: 'inline-flex',
                                    alignItems: 'center',
                                    gap: '6px',
                                    pointerEvents: 'none',
                                    zIndex: 5,
                                }}
                            >
                                <Maximize2 size={13} color="#5eead4" />
                                지도를 클릭하면 팝업창에서 마우스 휠 확대/축소가 가능합니다
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    )}
                </section>

                {/* 5. 객실 선택 섹션 */}
                <section style={{
                    backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                    borderRadius: '16px',
                    padding: '28px',
                    border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                    marginBottom: '24px'
                }}>
                    <div style={{
                        display: 'flex',
                        justifyContent: 'space-between',
                        alignItems: 'center',
                        flexWrap: 'wrap',
                        gap: '12px',
                        marginBottom: '20px'
                    }}>
                        <h2 style={{ fontSize: '18px', color: '#0f172a', margin: 0 }}>객실 선택</h2>

                        <div style={{
                            display: 'flex',
                            alignItems: 'center',
                            gap: '10px',
                            backgroundColor: '#f8fafc',
                            border: '1px solid #cbd5e1',
                            borderRadius: '12px',
                            padding: '8px 14px'
                        }}>
                            <Calendar size={16} color="#0f766e" />
                            <span style={{ fontSize: '12px', fontWeight: 600, color: '#475569' }}>조회 일정:</span>
                            <input
                                type="date"
                                min={todayStr}
                                value={checkIn}
                                onClick={(e) => (e.currentTarget as any).showPicker?.()}
                                onChange={(e) => {
                                    const nextIn = e.target.value;
                                    setCheckIn(nextIn);
                                    if (nextIn >= checkOut) {
                                        const nextD = new Date(nextIn);
                                        nextD.setDate(nextD.getDate() + 1);
                                        setCheckOut(nextD.toISOString().split('T')[0]);
                                    }
                                }}
                                style={{
                                    border: 'none',
                                    background: 'transparent',
                                    fontSize: '13px',
                                    fontWeight: 700,
                                    color: '#0f172a',
                                    cursor: 'pointer',
                                    outline: 'none'
                                }}
                            />
                            <span style={{ color: '#94a3b8' }}>~</span>
                            <input
                                type="date"
                                min={checkIn || todayStr}
                                value={checkOut}
                                onClick={(e) => (e.currentTarget as any).showPicker?.()}
                                onChange={(e) => setCheckOut(e.target.value)}
                                style={{
                                    border: 'none',
                                    background: 'transparent',
                                    fontSize: '13px',
                                    fontWeight: 700,
                                    color: '#0f172a',
                                    cursor: 'pointer',
                                    outline: 'none'
                                }}
                            />
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    {stay.rooms && stay.rooms.length > 0 ? (
                        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '28px' }}>
                            {stay.rooms.map((room) => (
                                <RoomCard
                                    key={room.roomId}
                                    room={room}
                                    stayTitle={stay.title}
                                    stayAddr={stay.addr}
                                    initialCheckIn={checkIn}
                                    initialCheckOut={checkOut}
                                    onPreviewImage={(url) => setRoomPreviewImage(url)}
                                />
                            ))}
                        </div>
                    ) : (
                        <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', padding: '36px 0', color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: '14px' }}>
                            등록된 개별 객실 정보가 없습니다.
                        </div>
                    )}
                </section>

                {/* 6. 주변 20km 관광지 슬라이더 */}
                {nearbyTours.length > 0 && (
                    <section style={{
                        backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                        borderRadius: '16px',
                        padding: '28px',
                        border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                        marginBottom: '24px',
                        position: 'relative'
                    }}>
                        <TourSliderSection
                            tours={nearbyTours}
                            headerRender={
                                <div>
                                    <span style={{
                                        display: 'inline-flex',
                                        alignItems: 'center',
                                        gap: '5px',
                                        fontSize: '12px',
                                        fontWeight: 800,
                                        color: '#0f766e',
                                        letterSpacing: '0.04em',
                                        marginBottom: '4px'
                                    }}>
                                        <Compass size={14} /> NEARBY ATTRACTIONS (20KM 이내)
                                    </span>
                                    <h2 style={{ fontSize: '19px', color: '#0f172a', margin: '0 0 4px 0', fontWeight: 800 }}>
                                        이 숙소에서 가까운 추천 관광지
                                    </h2>
                                    <p style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: '#64748b', margin: 0 }}>
                                        숙소 위치 기준 반경 20km 이내의 가까운 제주 명소 {nearbyTours.length}곳입니다.
                                    </p>
                                </div>
                            }
                        />
                    </section>
                )}

                {/* 7. 판매자 정보 아코디언 */}
                <section style={{
                    backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                    borderRadius: '16px',
                    padding: '20px 28px',
                    border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                    transition: 'all 0.2s ease',
                }}>
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setIsSellerInfoOpen((prev) => !prev)}
                        style={{
                            width: '100%',
                            display: 'flex',
                            justifyContent: 'space-between',
                            alignItems: 'center',
                            background: 'none',
                            border: 'none',
                            padding: 0,
                            cursor: 'pointer',
                        }}
                    >
                        <h2 style={{ fontSize: '16px', color: '#0f172a', margin: 0, fontWeight: 700 }}>
                            판매자 정보
                        </h2>
                        <ChevronDown
                            size={20}
                            color="#64748b"
                            style={{
                                transform: isSellerInfoOpen ? 'rotate(180deg)' : 'rotate(0deg)',
                                transition: 'transform 0.2s ease',
                            }}
                        />
                    </button>

                    {isSellerInfoOpen && (
                        <div style={{
                            marginTop: '16px',
                            paddingTop: '16px',
                            borderTop: '1px solid #f1f5f9',
                            display: 'flex',
                            flexDirection: 'column',
                            gap: '6px',
                            fontSize: '13px',
                            color: '#64748b'
                        }}>
                            <p style={{ margin: 0 }}>
                                상호명 / 대표자: <strong style={{ color: '#1e293b' }}>{stay.hostName}</strong>
                            </p>
                            <p style={{ margin: 0 }}>
                                사업자등록번호: {stay.businessNo || '등록된 사업자번호 없음'}
                            </p>
                            <p style={{ margin: 0 }}>
                                연락처: {stay.tel || '안내 번호 없음'}
                            </p>
                            <p style={{ margin: 0 }}>
                                사업자주소: {stay.addr}
                            </p>
                        </div>
                    )}
                </section>
            </main>

            <Footer />

            {/* 8. 공통 지도 크게 보기 팝업 모달 */}
            {stay.mapX && stay.mapY && (
                <LocationMapPopupModal
                    isOpen={isMapPopupOpen}
                    onClose={() => setIsMapPopupOpen(false)}
                    eyebrow="STAY LOCATION MAP"
                    title={stay.title}
                >
                    <KakaoStayMap
                        stays={[{ ...stay, basePrice: 0 } as any]}
                        mode="detail"
                        level={4}
                        height="100%"
                        zoomable={true}
                    />
                </LocationMapPopupModal>
            )}
        </div>
    );
}


// ==========================================
// File: src/pages/stay/WishlistPage.tsx
// ==========================================
import React, { useEffect, useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Heart, RotateCcw } from 'lucide-react';
import { fetchMyWishlist, toggleAccommodationLike } from '../../api/stays.ts';
import { Footer } from '../../components/common/Footer.tsx';
import { StayCard } from '../../components/stay/StayCard.tsx';
import { PageHeader} from "../../components/common/PageHeader.tsx";
import { WishlistDateFilterBar} from "../../components/stay/WishlistDateFilterBar.tsx";
import type { Stay } from '../../types/stay.ts';

export function WishlistPage() {
    const navigate = useNavigate();

    const todayStr = useMemo(() => new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0], []);

    const [checkIn, setCheckIn] = useState('');
    const [checkOut, setCheckOut] = useState('');
    const [guests, setGuests] = useState(2);

    const [wishlist, setWishlist] = useState<Stay[]>([]);
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
    const [removingIds, setRemovingIds] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());

    const loadWishlist = (targetIn?: string, targetOut?: string, targetGuests?: number) => {
        const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
        if (!token) {
            alert('로그인이 필요한 페이지입니다.');
            navigate('/login');
            return;
        }

        setLoading(true);
        fetchMyWishlist(targetIn, targetOut, targetGuests)
            .then((data) => setWishlist(data))
            .catch((err) => console.error(err))
            .finally(() => setLoading(false));
    };

    useEffect(() => {
        loadWishlist('', '', undefined);
    }, []);

    const handleSearch = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        loadWishlist(checkIn, checkOut, guests);
    };

    const handleResetFilter = () => {
        setCheckIn('');
        setCheckOut('');
        setGuests(2);
        loadWishlist('', '', undefined);
    };

    const handleRemoveLike = async (e: React.MouseEvent, contentId: string) => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        if (removingIds.has(contentId)) return;

        try {
            await toggleAccommodationLike(contentId);
            setRemovingIds((prev) => new Set(prev).add(contentId));

            setTimeout(() => {
                setWishlist((prev) => prev.filter((item) => item.contentId !== contentId));
                setRemovingIds((prev) => {
                    const next = new Set(prev);
                    next.delete(contentId);
                    return next;
                });
            }, 350);
        } catch {
            alert('위시리스트 삭제 중 오류가 발생했습니다.');
        }
    };

    return (
        <div style={{ backgroundColor: '#f8fafc', minHeight: '100vh', display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column' }}>
            {/* 1. 공통 상단 헤더 */}
            <PageHeader title="숙소 위시리스트" />

            <main className="container" style={{ maxWidth: '1000px', margin: '28px auto', padding: '0 20px', flex: 1, width: '100%' }}>
                {/* 2. 페이지 헤더 타이틀 */}
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'flex-end', marginBottom: '20px' }}>
                    <div>
                        <h1 style={{ fontSize: '22px', fontWeight: 800, color: '#0f172a', margin: '0 0 6px 0' }}>내가 찜한 제주 숙소</h1>
                        <p style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: '#64748b', margin: 0 }}>
                            {checkIn && checkOut
                                ? `${checkIn} ~ ${checkOut} 일정에 예약 가능한 찜한 숙소 목록입니다.`
                                : '찜해둔 숙소를 한눈에 비교하고 언제든 예약해 보세요.'}
                        </p>
                    </div>
                    {(checkIn || checkOut) && (
                        <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={handleResetFilter}
                            style={{
                                display: 'flex',
                                alignItems: 'center',
                                gap: '5px',
                                background: '#ffffff',
                                border: '1px solid #cbd5e1',
                                borderRadius: '20px',
                                padding: '6px 14px',
                                fontSize: '12px',
                                color: '#475569',
                                cursor: 'pointer',
                            }}
                        >
                            <RotateCcw size={13} /> 필터 초기화
                        </button>
                    )}
                </div>

                {/* 3. 분리된 일정 & 인원 검색 패널 */}
                <WishlistDateFilterBar
                    todayStr={todayStr}
                    checkIn={checkIn}
                    checkOut={checkOut}
                    guests={guests}
                    onCheckInChange={setCheckIn}
                    onCheckOutChange={setCheckOut}
                    onGuestsChange={setGuests}
                    onSearch={handleSearch}
                />

                {/* 4. 숙소 그리드 목록 */}
                {loading ? (
                    <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', padding: '80px 0', color: '#94a3b8' }}>목록을 불러오는 중입니다...</div>
                ) : wishlist.length === 0 ? (
                    <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', padding: '80px 0', backgroundColor: '#ffffff', borderRadius: '16px', border: '1px dashed #cbd5e1' }}>
                        <Heart size={44} color="#cbd5e1" style={{ marginBottom: '14px' }} />
                        <h3 style={{ fontSize: '16px', color: '#475569', margin: '0 0 8px 0' }}>
                            {checkIn && checkOut ? '해당 일정에 예약 가능한 찜한 숙소가 없습니다.' : '위시리스트가 비어 있습니다.'}
                        </h3>
                        <p style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: '#94a3b8', margin: '0 0 20px 0' }}>
                            {checkIn && checkOut ? '다른 날짜를 선택하시거나 필터를 초기화해 보세요.' : '마음에 드는 숙소를 찜해 두면 이곳에서 모아볼 수 있습니다.'}
                        </p>
                        {checkIn && checkOut ? (
                            <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={handleResetFilter}
                                style={{ padding: '10px 22px', backgroundColor: '#0f766e', color: '#ffffff', border: 'none', borderRadius: '24px', fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 600, cursor: 'pointer' }}
                            >
                                전체 찜 목록 보기
                            </button>
                        ) : (
                            <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => navigate('/')}
                                style={{ padding: '10px 22px', backgroundColor: '#123f4d', color: '#ffffff', border: 'none', borderRadius: '24px', fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 600, cursor: 'pointer' }}
                            >
                                숙소 둘러보기
                            </button>
                        )}
                    </div>
                ) : (
                    <div className="stay-grid">
                        {wishlist.map((s) => (
                            <StayCard
                                key={s.contentId}
                                stay={s}
                                isLiked={true}
                                isRemoving={removingIds.has(s.contentId)}
                                onCardClick={(stay) =>
                                    navigate(`/stays/${stay.contentId}`, {
                                        state: {
                                            checkIn: checkIn || '',
                                            checkOut: checkOut || '',
                                        },
                                    })
                                }
                                onToggleLike={handleRemoveLike}
                            />
                        ))}
                    </div>
                )}
            </main>

            <Footer />
        </div>
    );
}


// ==========================================
// File: src/pages/host/HostReservationsPage.tsx
// ==========================================
import { useEffect, useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import axios from 'axios';
import {
    Search,
    Building2,
    TrendingUp,
    CalendarCheck,
    CalendarX
} from 'lucide-react';
import { money } from '../../data/stays.ts';
import { Footer } from '../../components/common/Footer.tsx';
import { PageHeader} from "../../components/common/PageHeader.tsx";
import { HostReservationCard} from "../../components/host/HostReservationCard.tsx";

export interface HostReservationItem {
    reservationId: number;
    status: 'COMPLETED' | 'CANCELED' | string;
    checkInDate: string;
    checkOutDate: string;
    totalPrice: number;
    createdAt: string;

    guestId: number;
    guestName: string;
    guestEmail: string;

    contentId: string;
    stayTitle: string;
    firstImage: string;
    roomId: number;
    roomTitle: string;
    roomBaseCount: string;
    roomMaxCount: string;
}

type FilterTab = 'ALL' | 'UPCOMING' | 'PAST' | 'CANCELED';

export function HostReservationsPage() {
    const navigate = useNavigate();

    const [reservations, setReservations] = useState<HostReservationItem[]>([]);
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
    const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<FilterTab>('ALL');
    const [selectedStayId, setSelectedStayId] = useState<string>('ALL');
    const [searchKeyword, setSearchKeyword] = useState<string>('');
    const [cancelingId, setCancelingId] = useState<number | null>(null);

    const todayStr = useMemo(() => new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0], []);

    const loadHostReservations = () => {
        const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
        if (!token) {
            alert('로그인이 필요한 페이지입니다.');
            navigate('/login');
            return;
        }

        setLoading(true);
        axios.get<HostReservationItem[]>('/api/v1/reservations/host', {
            headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }
        })
            .then((res) => setReservations(res.data))
            .catch((err) => {
                console.error('호스트 예약 내역 조회 실패:', err);
                alert('예약 관리 목록을 불러오지 못했습니다.');
            })
            .finally(() => setLoading(false));
    };

    useEffect(() => {
        window.scrollTo(0, 0);
        loadHostReservations();
    }, []);

    const getCategory = (item: HostReservationItem): 'UPCOMING' | 'PAST' | 'CANCELED' => {
        if (item.status === 'CANCELED') return 'CANCELED';
        if (item.checkOutDate > todayStr) return 'UPCOMING';
        return 'PAST';
    };

    const stayOptions = useMemo(() => {
        const map = new Map<string, string>();
        reservations.forEach((r) => {
            if (!map.has(r.contentId)) map.set(r.contentId, r.stayTitle);
        });
        return Array.from(map.entries());
    }, [reservations]);

    const stats = useMemo(() => {
        let upcoming = 0;
        let past = 0;
        let canceled = 0;
        let totalRevenue = 0;

        reservations.forEach((r) => {
            const cat = getCategory(r);
            if (cat === 'UPCOMING') {
                upcoming++;
                totalRevenue += r.totalPrice || 0;
            } else if (cat === 'PAST') {
                past++;
                totalRevenue += r.totalPrice || 0;
            } else {
                canceled++;
            }
        });

        return {
            ALL: reservations.length,
            UPCOMING: upcoming,
            PAST: past,
            CANCELED: canceled,
            totalRevenue,
        };
    }, [reservations, todayStr]);

    const filteredList = useMemo(() => {
        return reservations.filter((item) => {
            if (activeTab !== 'ALL' && getCategory(item) !== activeTab) return false;
            if (selectedStayId !== 'ALL' && item.contentId !== selectedStayId) return false;
            if (searchKeyword.trim()) {
                const kw = searchKeyword.trim().toLowerCase();
                const matchGuest = item.guestName?.toLowerCase().includes(kw) || item.guestEmail?.toLowerCase().includes(kw);
                const matchRoom = item.roomTitle?.toLowerCase().includes(kw) || item.stayTitle?.toLowerCase().includes(kw);
                if (!matchGuest && !matchRoom) return false;
            }
            return true;
        });
    }, [reservations, activeTab, selectedStayId, searchKeyword, todayStr]);

    const handleHostCancel = async (item: HostReservationItem) => {
        const confirmCancel = window.confirm(
            `예약자 [${item.guestName}]님의 예약을 취소하시겠습니까?\n` +
            `· 숙소/객실: ${item.stayTitle} - ${item.roomTitle}\n` +
            `· 일정: ${item.checkInDate} ~ ${item.checkOutDate}\n\n` +
            `취소 시 되돌릴 수 없습니다.`
        );
        if (!confirmCancel) return;

        const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
        setCancelingId(item.reservationId);

        try {
            await axios.patch(`/api/v1/reservations/${item.reservationId}/cancel`, {}, {
                headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }
            });
            alert('해당 예약이 취소 처리되었습니다.');
            setReservations((prev) =>
                prev.map((r) =>
                    r.reservationId === item.reservationId ? { ...r, status: 'CANCELED' } : r
                )
            );
        } catch (err: any) {
            alert(err.response?.data?.message || err.response?.data || '예약 취소 처리 중 오류가 발생했습니다.');
        } finally {
            setCancelingId(null);
        }
    };

    return (
        <div style={{ backgroundColor: '#f8fafc', minHeight: '100vh', display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column' }}>
            {/* 1. 공통 상단 헤더 */}
            <PageHeader
                title="파트너 예약 내역 관리"
                rightElement={
                    <span style={{
                        fontSize: '12px',
                        backgroundColor: '#e6f4ea',
                        color: '#137333',
                        padding: '4px 10px',
                        borderRadius: '12px',
                        fontWeight: 600
                    }}>
                        HOST 파트너 센터
                    </span>
                }
            />

            <main className="container" style={{ maxWidth: '980px', margin: '28px auto', padding: '0 20px', flex: 1, width: '100%' }}>
                {/* 2. 상단 요약 대시보드 카드 */}
                <div style={{
                    display: 'grid',
                    gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fit, minmax(200px, 1fr))',
                    gap: '16px',
                    marginBottom: '24px'
                }}>
                    <div style={{ backgroundColor: '#ffffff', padding: '20px', borderRadius: '16px', border: '1px solid #e2e8f0', boxShadow: '0 2px 8px rgba(0,0,0,0.03)' }}>
                        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between', marginBottom: '8px' }}>
                            <span style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: '#64748b', fontWeight: 600 }}>예정·진행 중 예약</span>
                            <CalendarCheck size={18} color="#0f766e" />
                        </div>
                        <strong style={{ fontSize: '24px', color: '#0f172a', fontWeight: 800 }}>{stats.UPCOMING}건</strong>
                    </div>

                    <div style={{ backgroundColor: '#ffffff', padding: '20px', borderRadius: '16px', border: '1px solid #e2e8f0', boxShadow: '0 2px 8px rgba(0,0,0,0.03)' }}>
                        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between', marginBottom: '8px' }}>
                            <span style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: '#64748b', fontWeight: 600 }}>전체 누적 예약</span>
                            <Building2 size={18} color="#123f4d" />
                        </div>
                        <strong style={{ fontSize: '24px', color: '#0f172a', fontWeight: 800 }}>{stats.ALL}건</strong>
                        <span style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: '#94a3b8', marginLeft: '8px' }}>(취소 {stats.CANCELED}건)</span>
                    </div>

                    <div style={{ backgroundColor: '#ffffff', padding: '20px', borderRadius: '16px', border: '1px solid #e2e8f0', boxShadow: '0 2px 8px rgba(0,0,0,0.03)' }}>
                        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between', marginBottom: '8px' }}>
                            <span style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: '#64748b', fontWeight: 600 }}>확정 예약 누적 매출</span>
                            <TrendingUp size={18} color="#0f766e" />
                        </div>
                        <strong style={{ fontSize: '24px', color: '#0f766e', fontWeight: 800 }}>{money(stats.totalRevenue)}</strong>
                    </div>
                </div>

                {/* 3. 필터 탭 & 숙소/예약자 검색 바 */}
                <div style={{
                    display: 'flex',
                    justifyContent: 'space-between',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                    flexWrap: 'wrap',
                    gap: '12px',
                    marginBottom: '20px',
                    paddingBottom: '14px',
                    borderBottom: '1px solid #e2e8f0'
                }}>
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '8px', overflowX: 'auto' }}>
                        {[
                            { key: 'ALL', label: '전체', count: stats.ALL },
                            { key: 'UPCOMING', label: '투숙 예정·진행', count: stats.UPCOMING },
                            { key: 'PAST', label: '이용 완료', count: stats.PAST },
                            { key: 'CANCELED', label: '취소됨', count: stats.CANCELED },
                        ].map((tab) => {
                            const isActive = activeTab === tab.key;
                            return (
                                <button
                                    key={tab.key}
                                    type="button"
                                    onClick={() => setActiveTab(tab.key as FilterTab)}
                                    style={{
                                        padding: '8px 15px',
                                        borderRadius: '20px',
                                        border: isActive ? '1px solid #123f4d' : '1px solid #cbd5e1',
                                        backgroundColor: isActive ? '#123f4d' : '#ffffff',
                                        color: isActive ? '#ffffff' : '#475569',
                                        fontSize: '13px',
                                        fontWeight: 600,
                                        cursor: 'pointer',
                                        display: 'flex',
                                        alignItems: 'center',
                                        gap: '6px',
                                        whiteSpace: 'nowrap'
                                    }}
                                >
                                    <span>{tab.label}</span>
                                    <span style={{
                                        padding: '1px 7px',
                                        borderRadius: '10px',
                                        fontSize: '11px',
                                        backgroundColor: isActive ? 'rgba(255,255,255,0.2)' : '#f1f5f9',
                                        color: isActive ? '#ffffff' : '#64748b'
                                    }}>
                                        {tab.count}
                                    </span>
                                </button>
                            );
                        })}
                    </div>

                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '8px', alignItems: 'center', flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
                        {stayOptions.length > 1 && (
                            <select
                                value={selectedStayId}
                                onChange={(e) => setSelectedStayId(e.target.value)}
                                style={{
                                    padding: '8px 12px',
                                    borderRadius: '8px',
                                    border: '1px solid #cbd5e1',
                                    backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                                    fontSize: '13px',
                                    color: '#334155',
                                    fontWeight: 600,
                                    outline: 'none',
                                    cursor: 'pointer'
                                }}
                            >
                                <option value="ALL">모든 내 숙소 ({stayOptions.length})</option>
                                {stayOptions.map(([id, title]) => (
                                    <option key={id} value={id}>{title}</option>
                                ))}
                            </select>
                        )}

                        <div style={{ position: 'relative' }}>
                            <input
                                type="text"
                                value={searchKeyword}
                                onChange={(e) => setSearchKeyword(e.target.value)}
                                placeholder="예약자명, 이메일, 객실 검색"
                                style={{
                                    padding: '8px 12px 8px 34px',
                                    borderRadius: '8px',
                                    border: '1px solid #cbd5e1',
                                    backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                                    fontSize: '13px',
                                    width: '210px',
                                    outline: 'none'
                                }}
                            />
                            <Search size={15} color="#94a3b8" style={{ position: 'absolute', left: '11px', top: '10px' }} />
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>

                {/* 4. 예약 카드 리스트 */}
                {loading ? (
                    <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', padding: '80px 0', color: '#94a3b8' }}>
                        접수된 예약 내역을 불러오는 중입니다...
                    </div>
                ) : filteredList.length === 0 ? (
                    <div style={{
                        textAlign: 'center',
                        padding: '80px 20px',
                        backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                        borderRadius: '16px',
                        border: '1px dashed #cbd5e1'
                    }}>
                        <CalendarX size={44} color="#cbd5e1" style={{ marginBottom: '14px' }} />
                        <h3 style={{ fontSize: '16px', color: '#475569', margin: '0 0 8px 0' }}>
                            조건에 해당하는 예약 내역이 없습니다.
                        </h3>
                        <p style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: '#94a3b8', margin: 0 }}>
                            고객이 숙소를 예약하면 이곳에 실시간으로 표시됩니다.
                        </p>
                    </div>
                ) : (
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '16px' }}>
                        {filteredList.map((item) => (
                            <HostReservationCard
                                key={item.reservationId}
                                item={item}
                                category={getCategory(item)}
                                todayStr={todayStr}
                                canceling={cancelingId === item.reservationId}
                                onCancel={handleHostCancel}
                            />
                        ))}
                    </div>
                )}
            </main>

            <Footer />
        </div>
    );
}


// ==========================================
// File: src/pages/host/HostReviewsPage.tsx
// ==========================================
import { useEffect, useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { MessageSquare, Search } from 'lucide-react';
import { fetchHostReviews, type ReviewItem } from '../../api/reviews.ts';
import { Footer } from '../../components/common/Footer.tsx';
import { PageHeader} from "../../components/common/PageHeader.tsx";
import { HostReviewCard} from "../../components/host/HostReviewCard.tsx";

export function HostReviewsPage() {
    const navigate = useNavigate();

    const [reviews, setReviews] = useState<ReviewItem[]>([]);
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
    const [filterStatus, setFilterStatus] = useState<'ALL' | 'UNREPLIED' | 'REPLIED'>('ALL');
    const [selectedStay, setSelectedStay] = useState<string>('ALL');
    const [searchKeyword, setSearchKeyword] = useState('');

    const loadReviews = () => {
        const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
        if (!token) {
            alert('로그인이 필요합니다.');
            navigate('/login');
            return;
        }
        setLoading(true);
        fetchHostReviews()
            .then((data) => setReviews(data || []))
            .catch(() => alert('리뷰 목록을 불러오지 못했습니다.'))
            .finally(() => setLoading(false));
    };

    useEffect(() => {
        window.scrollTo(0, 0);
        loadReviews();
    }, []);

    const stayOptions = useMemo(() => {
        const map = new Map<string, string>();
        reviews.forEach((r) => map.set(r.contentId, r.stayTitle));
        return Array.from(map.entries());
    }, [reviews]);

    const filteredReviews = useMemo(() => {
        return reviews.filter((r) => {
            if (filterStatus === 'UNREPLIED' && r.hostReply) return false;
            if (filterStatus === 'REPLIED' && !r.hostReply) return false;
            if (selectedStay !== 'ALL' && r.contentId !== selectedStay) return false;
            if (searchKeyword.trim()) {
                const kw = searchKeyword.trim().toLowerCase();
                const match =
                    r.content?.toLowerCase().includes(kw) ||
                    r.authorName?.toLowerCase().includes(kw) ||
                    r.stayTitle?.toLowerCase().includes(kw);
                if (!match) return false;
            }
            return true;
        });
    }, [reviews, filterStatus, selectedStay, searchKeyword]);

    return (
        <div style={{ backgroundColor: '#f8fafc', minHeight: '100vh', display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column' }}>
            {/* 1. 공통 상단 헤더 */}
            <PageHeader
                title="숙소 리뷰 및 답글 관리"
                rightElement={
                    <span style={{
                        fontSize: '12px',
                        backgroundColor: '#e6f4ea',
                        color: '#137333',
                        padding: '4px 10px',
                        borderRadius: '12px',
                        fontWeight: 600
                    }}>
                        HOST 파트너 센터
                    </span>
                }
            />

            <main className="container" style={{ maxWidth: '940px', margin: '28px auto', padding: '0 20px', flex: 1, width: '100%' }}>
                {/* 2. 상단 필터 및 검색 바 */}
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: '12px', marginBottom: '20px', paddingBottom: '14px', borderBottom: '1px solid #e2e8f0' }}>
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '8px' }}>
                        {[
                            { key: 'ALL', label: `전체 리뷰 (${reviews.length})` },
                            { key: 'UNREPLIED', label: `미답변 (${reviews.filter((r) => !r.hostReply).length})` },
                            { key: 'REPLIED', label: `답변 완료 (${reviews.filter((r) => r.hostReply).length})` },
                        ].map((tab) => {
                            const active = filterStatus === tab.key;
                            return (
                                <button
                                    key={tab.key}
                                    type="button"
                                    onClick={() => setFilterStatus(tab.key as 'ALL' | 'UNREPLIED' | 'REPLIED')}
                                    style={{
                                        padding: '8px 16px',
                                        borderRadius: '20px',
                                        border: active ? '1px solid #123f4d' : '1px solid #cbd5e1',
                                        backgroundColor: active ? '#123f4d' : '#ffffff',
                                        color: active ? '#ffffff' : '#475569',
                                        fontSize: '13px',
                                        fontWeight: 600,
                                        cursor: 'pointer'
                                    }}
                                >
                                    {tab.label}
                                </button>
                            );
                        })}
                    </div>

                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '8px', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                        {stayOptions.length > 1 && (
                            <select
                                value={selectedStay}
                                onChange={(e) => setSelectedStay(e.target.value)}
                                style={{ padding: '8px 12px', borderRadius: '8px', border: '1px solid #cbd5e1', fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 600 }}
                            >
                                <option value="ALL">모든 내 숙소</option>
                                {stayOptions.map(([id, title]) => (
                                    <option key={id} value={id}>{title}</option>
                                ))}
                            </select>
                        )}
                        <div style={{ position: 'relative' }}>
                            <input
                                type="text"
                                value={searchKeyword}
                                onChange={(e) => setSearchKeyword(e.target.value)}
                                placeholder="작성자, 리뷰 내용 검색"
                                style={{ padding: '8px 12px 8px 34px', borderRadius: '8px', border: '1px solid #cbd5e1', fontSize: '13px', width: '200px' }}
                            />
                            <Search size={15} color="#94a3b8" style={{ position: 'absolute', left: '11px', top: '10px' }} />
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>

                {/* 3. 리뷰 목록 */}
                {loading ? (
                    <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', padding: '80px 0', color: '#94a3b8' }}>리뷰 내역을 불러오는 중입니다...</div>
                ) : filteredReviews.length === 0 ? (
                    <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', padding: '80px 20px', backgroundColor: '#ffffff', borderRadius: '16px', border: '1px dashed #cbd5e1' }}>
                        <MessageSquare size={44} color="#cbd5e1" style={{ marginBottom: '12px' }} />
                        <h3 style={{ fontSize: '16px', color: '#475569', margin: '0 0 6px 0' }}>등록된 리뷰가 없습니다.</h3>
                        <p style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: '#94a3b8', margin: 0 }}>투숙객이 리뷰를 남기면 이곳에서 확인하고 답글을 달 수 있습니다.</p>
                    </div>
                ) : (
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '16px' }}>
                        {filteredReviews.map((rev) => (
                            <HostReviewCard
                                key={rev.reviewId}
                                review={rev}
                                onRefresh={loadReviews}
                            />
                        ))}
                    </div>
                )}
            </main>

            <Footer />
        </div>
    );
}


// ==========================================
// File: src/pages/host/HostAccommodationPage.tsx
// ==========================================
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import axios from 'axios';
import {
    MapPin,
    Search,
    CheckSquare,
    CheckCircle2,
    Clock,
    XCircle
} from 'lucide-react';
import { Footer } from '../../components/common/Footer.tsx';
import { PageHeader} from "../../components/common/PageHeader.tsx";
import { HostRoomManager } from '../../components/host/HostRoomManager.tsx';

declare const window: typeof globalThis & {
    kakao: any;
};

const FACILITY_OPTIONS = [
    '와이파이', '수영장', '바비큐장', '조식 제공', '피트니스',
    '무료 주차', '편의점', '레스토랑', '스파/사우나', '카페', '반려동물 동반'
];

const TIME_OPTIONS = Array.from({ length: 14 }, (_, i) => {
    const hour = i + 11;
    return `${hour}:00`;
});

interface MyAccommodation {
    contentId: string;
    title: string;
    lodgingType: string;
    tel: string;
    zipcode: string;
    addr: string;
    checkinTime: string;
    checkoutTime: string;
    roomCount: string;
    roomType: string;
    parking: string;
    subFacility: string;
    overview: string;
    firstImage: string;
    firstImage2: string;
    mapX: string;
    mapY: string;
    approvalStatus: 'PENDING' | 'APPROVED' | 'REJECTED';
    rejectReason?: string;
}

export function HostAccommodationPage() {
    const [myAccommodations, setMyAccommodations] = useState<MyAccommodation[]>([]);
    const [selectedContentId, setSelectedContentId] = useState<string | null>(null);
    const [approvalStatus, setApprovalStatus] = useState<'PENDING' | 'APPROVED' | 'REJECTED' | null>(null);
    const [rejectReason, setRejectReason] = useState<string | null>(null);

    const [title, setTitle] = useState('');
    const [lodgingType, setLodgingType] = useState('AC01');
    const [tel, setTel] = useState('');
    const [zipcode, setZipcode] = useState('');
    const [addr, setAddr] = useState('');
    const [checkinTime, setCheckinTime] = useState('15:00');
    const [checkoutTime, setCheckoutTime] = useState('11:00');
    const [roomCount, setRoomCount] = useState('');
    const [roomType, setRoomType] = useState('');
    const [parking, setParking] = useState('');
    const [overview, setOverview] = useState('');
    const [firstImage, setFirstImage] = useState('');
    const [firstImage2, setFirstImage2] = useState('');
    const [selectedFacilities, setSelectedFacilities] = useState<string[]>([]);
    const [mapX, setMapX] = useState('');
    const [mapY, setMapY] = useState('');

    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
    const [initialLoading, setInitialLoading] = useState(true);

    const isEditMode = Boolean(selectedContentId);

    useEffect(() => {
        window.scrollTo(0, 0);
        fetchMyAccommodationList();
    }, []);

    // 카카오 지도 SDK 로드 (주소 -> 경위도 자동 검색용)
    useEffect(() => {
        const loadKakaoApi = async () => {
            if (document.getElementById('kakao-map-sdk')) return;

            try {
                let appKey = import.meta.env.VITE_KAKAO_MAP_KEY || '';
                if (!appKey) {
                    const res = await axios.get('/api/v1/accommodations/map-key');
                    appKey = res.data.mapKey;
                }

                if (appKey) {
                    const script = document.createElement('script');
                    script.id = 'kakao-map-sdk';
                    script.src = `//dapi.kakao.com/v2/maps/sdk.js?appkey=${appKey}&libraries=clusterer,services&autoload=false`;
                    script.async = true;
                    document.head.appendChild(script);
                }
            } catch (err) {
                console.error('카카오 키 로드 실패', err);
            }
        };

        loadKakaoApi();
    }, []);

    const fetchMyAccommodationList = async (targetId?: string) => {
        const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
        if (!token) {
            setInitialLoading(false);
            return;
        }

        try {
            setInitialLoading(true);
            const res = await axios.get<MyAccommodation[]>('/api/v1/accommodations/my', {
                headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }
            });
            const list = res.data || [];
            setMyAccommodations(list);

            if (list.length > 0) {
                const target =
                    (targetId && list.find((a) => a.contentId === targetId)) ||
                    list.find((a) => a.approvalStatus === 'APPROVED') ||
                    list[0];
                populateFormWithAccommodation(target);
            } else {
                resetToCreateMode();
            }
        } catch (err) {
            console.error('내 숙소 목록 조회 실패:', err);
        } finally {
            setInitialLoading(false);
        }
    };

    const populateFormWithAccommodation = (accom: MyAccommodation) => {
        setSelectedContentId(accom.contentId);
        setApprovalStatus(accom.approvalStatus);
        setRejectReason(accom.rejectReason || null);

        setTitle(accom.title || '');
        setLodgingType(accom.lodgingType || 'AC01');
        setTel(accom.tel || '');
        setZipcode(accom.zipcode || '');
        setAddr(accom.addr || '');
        setCheckinTime(accom.checkinTime || '15:00');
        setCheckoutTime(accom.checkoutTime || '11:00');
        setRoomCount(accom.roomCount || '');
        setRoomType(accom.roomType || '');
        setParking(accom.parking || '');
        setOverview(accom.overview || '');
        setFirstImage(accom.firstImage || '');
        setFirstImage2(accom.firstImage2 || '');
        setMapX(accom.mapX || '');
        setMapY(accom.mapY || '');

        const facArray = accom.subFacility
            ? accom.subFacility.split(',').map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean)
            : [];
        setSelectedFacilities(facArray);
    };

    const resetToCreateMode = () => {
        setSelectedContentId(null);
        setApprovalStatus(null);
        setRejectReason(null);
        setTitle('');
        setLodgingType('AC01');
        setTel('');
        setZipcode('');
        setAddr('');
        setCheckinTime('15:00');
        setCheckoutTime('11:00');
        setRoomCount('');
        setRoomType('');
        setParking('');
        setSelectedFacilities([]);
        setOverview('');
        setFirstImage('');
        setFirstImage2('');
        setMapX('');
        setMapY('');
    };

    const handleFacilityToggle = (facility: string) => {
        setSelectedFacilities((prev) =>
            prev.includes(facility)
                ? prev.filter((f) => f !== facility)
                : [...prev, facility]
        );
    };

    const handleSearchGeocode = () => {
        if (!addr.trim()) {
            alert('먼저 주소를 입력해 주세요.');
            return;
        }

        if (!window.kakao || !window.kakao.maps) {
            alert('카카오 지도 서비스가 아직 로드되지 않았습니다. 새로고침 후 다시 시도해 주세요.');
            return;
        }

        window.kakao.maps.load(() => {
            if (!window.kakao.maps.services) {
                alert('카카오맵 services를 불러오지 못했습니다.');
                return;
            }

            const geocoder = new window.kakao.maps.services.Geocoder();
            geocoder.addressSearch(addr, (result: any, status: any) => {
                if (status === window.kakao.maps.services.Status.OK) {
                    setMapX(result[0].x);
                    setMapY(result[0].y);
                    alert('경위도 좌표가 성공적으로 자동 입력되었습니다.');
                } else {
                    alert('해당 주소의 좌표를 찾을 수 없습니다. 주소를 더 정확히 입력해 주세요. (예: 제주특별자치도 서귀포시 중문관광로 72)');
                }
            });
        });
    };

    const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
        e.preventDefault();

        if (!mapX || !mapY) {
            alert('주소 옆의 [좌표 자동 검색] 버튼을 눌러 경위도 좌표를 채워주세요.');
            return;
        }

        setLoading(true);
        const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');

        const payload = {
            title,
            lodgingType,
            tel,
            zipcode,
            addr,
            checkinTime,
            checkoutTime,
            roomCount,
            roomType: roomType || '',
            parking,
            subFacility: selectedFacilities.join(', '),
            overview,
            firstImage,
            firstImage2,
            mapX,
            mapY
        };

        try {
            if (isEditMode && selectedContentId) {
                await axios.put(`/api/v1/accommodations/${selectedContentId}`, payload, {
                    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }
                });
                alert('숙소 정보가 성공적으로 수정되었습니다.');
                await fetchMyAccommodationList(selectedContentId);
            } else {
                const res = await axios.post('/api/v1/accommodations', payload, {
                    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }
                });
                const newId = typeof res.data === 'string' ? res.data : undefined;
                alert('숙소 등록 신청이 완료되었습니다. 아래에서 객실(룸)도 미리 등록하실 수 있습니다.');
                await fetchMyAccommodationList(newId);
            }
        } catch (err: any) {
            alert(err.response?.data?.message || '숙소 처리 중 오류가 발생했습니다.');
        } finally {
            setLoading(false);
        }
    };

    const handleDeleteAccommodation = async () => {
        if (!selectedContentId) return;
        if (!window.confirm('정말 이 숙소를 삭제하시겠습니까? 등록된 객실 정보도 함께 삭제됩니다.')) return;

        const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
        try {
            await axios.delete(`/api/v1/accommodations/${selectedContentId}`, {
                headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }
            });
            alert('숙소가 정상적으로 삭제되었습니다.');
            await fetchMyAccommodationList();
        } catch (err: any) {
            alert(err.response?.data?.message || '숙소 삭제 중 오류가 발생했습니다.');
        }
    };

    return (
        <div style={{ backgroundColor: '#f8fafc', minHeight: '100vh', display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column' }}>
            <style>{`
                .host-input::placeholder { color: #cbd5e1 !important; font-weight: 400; }
                .host-input:focus { border-color: #0f766e !important; box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px rgba(15, 118, 110, 0.1) !important; outline: none; }
            `}</style>

            {/* 1. 공통 상단 헤더 + 우측 숙소 선택 셀렉트 박스 */}
            <PageHeader
                title={isEditMode ? '내 숙소 및 객실 관리' : '신규 숙소 등록 신청'}
                rightElement={
                    myAccommodations.length > 0 ? (
                        <select
                            value={selectedContentId || 'NEW'}
                            onChange={(e) => {
                                if (e.target.value === 'NEW') {
                                    resetToCreateMode();
                                } else {
                                    const found = myAccommodations.find((a) => a.contentId === e.target.value);
                                    if (found) populateFormWithAccommodation(found);
                                }
                            }}
                            style={{
                                padding: '8px 12px',
                                borderRadius: '8px',
                                border: '1px solid #cbd5e1',
                                fontSize: '13px',
                                fontWeight: 600,
                                color: '#123f4d',
                                cursor: 'pointer'
                            }}
                        >
                            {myAccommodations.map((item) => (
                                <option key={item.contentId} value={item.contentId}>
                                    [{item.approvalStatus === 'APPROVED' ? '승인완료' : item.approvalStatus === 'REJECTED' ? '반려됨' : '심사대기'}] {item.title}
                                </option>
                            ))}
                            <option value="NEW">+ 새 숙소 추가 신청하기</option>
                        </select>
                    ) : undefined
                }
            />

            <main className="container" style={{ maxWidth: '760px', margin: '32px auto', padding: '0 20px', flex: 1, width: '100%' }}>
                {initialLoading ? (
                    <div style={{ padding: '60px 0', textAlign: 'center', color: '#64748b' }}>숙소 정보를 불러오는 중입니다...</div>
                ) : (
                    <>
                        {/* 수정 모드일 때 상단 승인 상태 배너 및 숙소 삭제 버튼 */}
                        {isEditMode && approvalStatus && (
                            <div style={{
                                marginBottom: '20px',
                                padding: '16px 20px',
                                borderRadius: '14px',
                                display: 'flex',
                                alignItems: 'center',
                                justifyContent: 'space-between',
                                flexWrap: 'wrap',
                                gap: '12px',
                                backgroundColor: approvalStatus === 'APPROVED' ? '#f0fdf4' : approvalStatus === 'REJECTED' ? '#fef2f2' : '#fffbeb',
                                border: `1px solid ${approvalStatus === 'APPROVED' ? '#bbf7d0' : approvalStatus === 'REJECTED' ? '#fecaca' : '#fde68a'}`
                            }}>
                                <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '10px' }}>
                                    {approvalStatus === 'APPROVED' && <CheckCircle2 size={20} color="#16a34a" />}
                                    {approvalStatus === 'PENDING' && <Clock size={20} color="#d97706" />}
                                    {approvalStatus === 'REJECTED' && <XCircle size={20} color="#dc2626" />}
                                    <div>
                                        <strong style={{ fontSize: '14px', color: '#0f172a', display: 'block' }}>
                                            {approvalStatus === 'APPROVED' && '승인 완료된 숙소입니다 (현재 메인 노출 중)'}
                                            {approvalStatus === 'PENDING' && '관리자 승인 심사 대기 중인 숙소입니다'}
                                            {approvalStatus === 'REJECTED' && `반려된 숙소입니다 (사유: ${rejectReason || '관리자 문의'})`}
                                        </strong>
                                        <span style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: '#475569' }}>
                                            숙소 정보를 수정하거나 페이지 하단에서 객실(Room)을 등록·관리할 수 있습니다.
                                        </span>
                                    </div>
                                </div>

                                <button
                                    type="button"
                                    onClick={handleDeleteAccommodation}
                                    style={{
                                        padding: '7px 13px',
                                        backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                                        color: '#dc2626',
                                        border: '1px solid #fecaca',
                                        borderRadius: '8px',
                                        fontSize: '12px',
                                        fontWeight: 600,
                                        cursor: 'pointer'
                                    }}
                                >
                                    숙소 삭제
                                </button>
                            </div>
                        )}

                        {/* 숙소 기본 정보 등록 / 수정 폼 */}
                        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} style={{ backgroundColor: '#ffffff', borderRadius: '16px', padding: '32px', border: '1px solid #e2e8f0', boxShadow: '0 4px 16px rgba(0,0,0,0.04)', display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '28px' }}>
                            <div>
                                <h2 style={{ fontSize: '18px', fontWeight: 800, color: '#0f172a', margin: '0 0 6px 0' }}>1. 기본 정보</h2>
                                <p style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: '#64748b', margin: '0 0 16px 0' }}>여행객에게 보여질 숙소의 기본적인 정보를 입력해주세요.</p>

                                <div style={{ display: 'grid', gap: '16px' }}>
                                    <div>
                                        <label style={labelStyle}>숙소 이름 <span style={{ color: '#e11d48' }}>*</span></label>
                                        <input type="text" required value={title} onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)} placeholder="예: 신라호텔 제주" className="host-input" style={inputStyle} />
                                    </div>
                                    <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: '16px' }}>
                                        <div>
                                            <label style={labelStyle}>숙소 종류 <span style={{ color: '#e11d48' }}>*</span></label>
                                            <select value={lodgingType} onChange={(e) => setLodgingType(e.target.value)} className="host-input" style={{ ...inputStyle, cursor: 'pointer' }}>
                                                <option value="AC01">호텔</option>
                                                <option value="AC02">콘도/리조트</option>
                                                <option value="AC03">펜션/풀빌라</option>
                                                <option value="AC04">모텔</option>
                                                <option value="AC05">캠핑/글램핑</option>
                                                <option value="AC06">게스트하우스</option>
                                            </select>
                                        </div>
                                        <div>
                                            <label style={labelStyle}>숙소 대표 연락처 <span style={{ color: '#e11d48' }}>*</span></label>
                                            <input type="text" required value={tel} onChange={(e) => setTel(e.target.value)} placeholder="예: 064-123-4567" className="host-input" style={inputStyle} />
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                            </div>

                            <div style={{ borderTop: '1px solid #f1f5f9', paddingTop: '28px' }}>
                                <h2 style={{ fontSize: '18px', fontWeight: 800, color: '#0f172a', margin: '0 0 6px 0' }}>2. 위치 및 좌표 정보</h2>
                                <p style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: '#64748b', margin: '0 0 16px 0' }}>상세 주소를 입력한 뒤 <strong>[좌표 자동 검색]</strong> 버튼을 꼭 눌러주세요.</p>

                                <div style={{ display: 'grid', gap: '16px' }}>
                                    <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '100px 1fr', gap: '12px' }}>
                                        <div>
                                            <label style={labelStyle}>우편번호</label>
                                            <input type="text" value={zipcode} onChange={(e) => setZipcode(e.target.value)} placeholder="63535" className="host-input" style={inputStyle} />
                                        </div>
                                        <div>
                                            <label style={labelStyle}>상세 주소 <span style={{ color: '#e11d48' }}>*</span></label>
                                            <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '8px' }}>
                                                <input type="text" required value={addr} onChange={(e) => setAddr(e.target.value)} placeholder="예: 제주특별자치도 서귀포시 중문관광로 72" className="host-input" style={{ ...inputStyle, flex: 1 }} />
                                                <button type="button" onClick={handleSearchGeocode} style={{ padding: '0 16px', backgroundColor: '#f8fafc', border: '1px solid #cbd5e1', borderRadius: '8px', fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 700, color: '#0f766e', cursor: 'pointer', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>
                                                    <Search size={14} style={{ display: 'inline', marginRight: '4px', verticalAlign: 'text-bottom' }} /> 좌표 자동 검색
                                                </button>
                                            </div>
                                        </div>
