// ==========================================
// File: src/pages/reservation/PaymentSuccessPage.tsx
// ==========================================
import { useEffect, useState, useRef } from 'react';
import { useNavigate, useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import axios from 'axios';
import { CheckCircle2, AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import { Footer } from '../../components/common/Footer.tsx';

export function PaymentSuccessPage() {
    const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const [status, setStatus] = useState<'loading' | 'success' | 'error'>('loading');
    const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState('');

    // React strict mode에서 API가 두 번 호출되는 것을 막기 위한 방어 로직
    const isProcessed = useRef(false);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (isProcessed.current) return;
        isProcessed.current = true;

        // 1. 토스가 자동으로 넘겨주는 결제 검증 데이터
        const paymentKey = searchParams.get('paymentKey');
        const orderId = searchParams.get('orderId');
        const amount = searchParams.get('amount');

        // 2. 우리가 결제 요청 시 붙여서 보냈던 객실 예약 데이터
        const roomId = searchParams.get('roomId');
        const checkIn = searchParams.get('checkIn');
        const checkOut = searchParams.get('checkOut');

        if (!paymentKey || !roomId || !amount) {
            setStatus('error');
            setErrorMessage('잘못된 접근입니다. 결제 정보가 누락되었습니다.');
            return;
        }

        const confirmPaymentAndReserve = async () => {
            try {
                const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');

                // 새로운 PaymentConfirmRequest DTO 규격에 맞춤
                const requestBody = {
                    paymentKey: paymentKey,
                    orderId: orderId,
                    amount: Number(amount),
                    roomId: Number(roomId),
                    checkInDate: checkIn,
                    checkOutDate: checkOut,
                };

                // 💡 임시 예약 API 대신 새로 만든 진짜 결제 검증 API로 전송!
                await axios.post('/api/v1/payments/confirm', requestBody, {
                    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }
                });

                setStatus('success');

            } catch (err: any) {
                console.error(err);
                setStatus('error');
                // 백엔드에서 날려준 에러 메시지(환불 완료 등)를 화면에 표시
                setErrorMessage(err.response?.data || '결제 및 예약 처리 중 오류가 발생했습니다.');
            }
        };

        confirmPaymentAndReserve();
    }, [searchParams]);

    return (
        <div style={{ backgroundColor: '#f8fafc', minHeight: '100vh', display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', justifyContent: 'space-between' }}>
            <main style={{ flex: 1, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', padding: '20px' }}>
                <div style={{ backgroundColor: '#ffffff', padding: '40px', borderRadius: '16px', boxShadow: '0 4px 20px rgba(0,0,0,0.05)', textAlign: 'center', maxWidth: '400px', width: '100%' }}>

                    {status === 'loading' && (
                        <>
                            <div className="spinner" style={{ width: '40px', height: '40px', border: '4px solid #e2e8f0', borderTopColor: '#0f766e', borderRadius: '50%', animation: 'spin 1s linear infinite', margin: '0 auto 20px' }} />
                            <h2 style={{ fontSize: '20px', color: '#0f172a', marginBottom: '8px' }}>결제 승인 중...</h2>
                            <p style={{ color: '#64748b', fontSize: '14px' }}>창을 닫거나 새로고침하지 마세요.</p>
                        </>
                    )}

                    {status === 'success' && (
                        <>
                            <CheckCircle2 size={56} color="#0f766e" style={{ margin: '0 auto 20px' }} />
                            <h2 style={{ fontSize: '22px', color: '#0f172a', marginBottom: '12px' }}>결제 및 예약 완료!</h2>
                            <p style={{ color: '#64748b', fontSize: '14px', lineHeight: 1.5, marginBottom: '24px' }}>
                                안전하게 예약이 확정되었습니다.<br />
                                즐거운 제주 여행 되세요!
                            </p>
                            <button onClick={() => navigate('/')} style={{ padding: '12px 24px', backgroundColor: '#123f4d', color: '#fff', border: 'none', borderRadius: '8px', fontWeight: 600, cursor: 'pointer', width: '100%' }}>
                                홈으로 돌아가기
                            </button>
                        </>
                    )}

                    {status === 'error' && (
                        <>
                            <AlertCircle size={56} color="#dc2626" style={{ margin: '0 auto 20px' }} />
                            <h2 style={{ fontSize: '22px', color: '#0f172a', marginBottom: '12px' }}>예약 실패</h2>
                            <p style={{ color: '#64748b', fontSize: '14px', lineHeight: 1.5, marginBottom: '24px', wordBreak: 'keep-all' }}>
                                {errorMessage}
                            </p>
                            <button onClick={() => navigate('/')} style={{ padding: '12px 24px', backgroundColor: '#f1f5f9', color: '#475569', border: 'none', borderRadius: '8px', fontWeight: 600, cursor: 'pointer', width: '100%' }}>
                                메인으로 돌아가기
                            </button>
                        </>
                    )}

                    {/* 스피너 CSS (간단한 인라인용) */}
                    <style>{`@keyframes spin { 0% { transform: rotate(0deg); } 100% { transform: rotate(360deg); } }`}</style>
                </div>
            </main>
            <Footer />
        </div>
    );
}


// ==========================================
// File: src/pages/reservation/ReservationPage.tsx
// ==========================================
import React, { useState, useEffect, useMemo, useRef } from 'react';
import { useLocation, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Calendar, MapPin, User, ShieldCheck } from 'lucide-react';
import { loadPaymentWidget } from '@tosspayments/payment-widget-sdk';
import { money } from '../../data/stays.ts';
import { Footer } from '../../components/common/Footer.tsx';
import { PageHeader} from "../../components/common/PageHeader.tsx";

interface ReservationState {
    roomId: number;
    roomTitle: string;
    pricePerNight: number;
    roomImage: string;
    stayTitle: string;
    stayAddr: string;
    checkIn?: string;
    checkOut?: string;
}

export function ReservationPage() {
    const location = useLocation();
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const roomState = location.state as ReservationState;

    const todayStr = useMemo(() => new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0], []);
    const tomorrowStr = useMemo(() => {
        const d = new Date();
        d.setDate(d.getDate() + 1);
        return d.toISOString().split('T')[0];
    }, []);

    const [checkInDate, setCheckInDate] = useState(roomState?.checkIn || todayStr);
    const [checkOutDate, setCheckOutDate] = useState(roomState?.checkOut || tomorrowStr);
    const [guestName, setGuestName] = useState('');
    const [phone, setPhone] = useState('');

    // 토스페이먼츠 위젯 상태 관리
    const paymentWidgetRef = useRef<Awaited<ReturnType<typeof loadPaymentWidget>> | null>(null);
    const paymentMethodsWidgetRef = useRef<any>(null);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (!roomState || !roomState.roomId) {
            alert('잘못된 접근입니다. 숙소를 먼저 선택해 주세요.');
            navigate(-1);
            return;
        }

        const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
        if (!token) {
            alert('로그인이 필요합니다.');
            navigate('/login');
            return;
        }

        try {
            const base64Payload = token.split('.')[1];
            const decoded = JSON.parse(
                decodeURIComponent(
                    atob(base64Payload)
                        .split('')
                        .map((c) => '%' + ('00' + c.charCodeAt(0).toString(16)).slice(-2))
                        .join('')
                )
            );
            if (decoded.name) setGuestName(decoded.name);
        } catch {}
    }, [roomState, navigate]);

    const nights = useMemo(() => {
        if (!checkInDate || !checkOutDate) return 0;
        const start = new Date(checkInDate);
        const end = new Date(checkOutDate);
        const diff = (end.getTime() - start.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24);
        return diff > 0 ? diff : 0;
    }, [checkInDate, checkOutDate]);

    const totalPrice = useMemo(() => {
        if (!roomState?.pricePerNight || nights <= 0) return 0;
        return roomState.pricePerNight * nights;
    }, [roomState, nights]);

    // 토스 결제 위젯 렌더링 로직
    useEffect(() => {
        (async () => {
            try {
                const res = await fetch('/api/v1/payments/client-key');
                const data = await res.json();
                const clientKey = data?.clientKey;

                if (!clientKey) {
                    console.error('토스 클라이언트 키를 불러오지 못했습니다. application-secret.properties 설정을 확인하세요.');
                    return;
                }

                const customerKey = 'customer_' + new Date().getTime();
                const paymentWidget = await loadPaymentWidget(clientKey, customerKey);

                const paymentMethodsWidget = paymentWidget.renderPaymentMethods(
                    '#payment-widget',
                    { value: totalPrice },
                    { variantKey: 'DEFAULT' }
                );

                paymentWidget.renderAgreement('#agreement', { variantKey: 'AGREEMENT' });

                paymentWidgetRef.current = paymentWidget;
                paymentMethodsWidgetRef.current = paymentMethodsWidget;
            } catch (err) {
                console.error('결제 위젯 로딩 실패:', err);
            }
        })();
    }, []);

    // 결제 금액이 바뀔 때 위젯 업데이트
    useEffect(() => {
        const paymentMethodsWidget = paymentMethodsWidgetRef.current;
        if (paymentMethodsWidget) {
            paymentMethodsWidget.updateAmount(totalPrice);
        }
    }, [totalPrice]);

    // 결제 요청 로직
    const handlePaymentRequest = async (e: React.SyntheticEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
        e.preventDefault();

        if (nights <= 0) {
            alert('체크아웃 날짜는 체크인 날짜 이후여야 합니다.');
            return;
        }

        const paymentWidget = paymentWidgetRef.current;
        try {
            await paymentWidget?.requestPayment({
                orderId: `ORDER_${new Date().getTime()}`,
                orderName: `${roomState.stayTitle} - ${roomState.roomTitle}`,
                customerName: guestName,
                customerEmail: 'customer@example.com',
                successUrl: `${window.location.origin}/payment/success?roomId=${roomState.roomId}&checkIn=${checkInDate}&checkOut=${checkOutDate}`,
                failUrl: `${window.location.origin}/payment/fail`,
            });
        } catch (err) {
            console.error('결제 실패:', err);
        }
    };

    if (!roomState) return null;

    return (
        <div style={{ backgroundColor: '#f8fafc', minHeight: '100vh' }}>
            {/* 공통 상단 헤더 */}
            <PageHeader title="예약 및 결제" titleSize="16px" />

            <main className="container" style={{ maxWidth: '960px', margin: '32px auto', padding: '0 16px' }}>
                <form onSubmit={handlePaymentRequest} style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1.4fr 1fr', gap: '24px' }}>
                    {/* 좌측: 일정 선택 및 예약자 정보 */}
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '20px' }}>
                        <div style={{ backgroundColor: '#ffffff', borderRadius: '16px', padding: '24px', border: '1px solid #e2e8f0' }}>
                            <h3 style={{ fontSize: '17px', fontWeight: 700, color: '#0f172a', margin: '0 0 16px 0', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px' }}>
                                <Calendar size={18} color="#0f766e" /> 일정 선택
                            </h3>
                            <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: '14px' }}>
                                <div>
                                    <label style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 600, color: '#475569', marginBottom: '6px' }}>체크인</label>
                                    <input
                                        type="date"
                                        min={todayStr}
                                        value={checkInDate}
                                        onChange={(e) => setCheckInDate(e.target.value)}
                                        style={{ width: '100%', padding: '10px 12px', borderRadius: '8px', border: '1px solid #cbd5e1', fontSize: '14px' }}
                                        required
                                    />
                                </div>
                                <div>
                                    <label style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 600, color: '#475569', marginBottom: '6px' }}>체크아웃</label>
                                    <input
                                        type="date"
                                        min={checkInDate}
                                        value={checkOutDate}
                                        onChange={(e) => setCheckOutDate(e.target.value)}
                                        style={{ width: '100%', padding: '10px 12px', borderRadius: '8px', border: '1px solid #cbd5e1', fontSize: '14px' }}
                                        required
                                    />
                                </div>
                            </div>
                            <div style={{ marginTop: '12px', fontSize: '13px', color: '#0f766e', fontWeight: 600 }}>
                                📅 총 {nights}박 {nights + 1}일 일정
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        <div style={{ backgroundColor: '#ffffff', borderRadius: '16px', padding: '24px', border: '1px solid #e2e8f0' }}>
                            <h3 style={{ fontSize: '17px', fontWeight: 700, color: '#0f172a', margin: '0 0 16px 0', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px' }}>
                                <User size={18} color="#0f766e" /> 예약자 정보
                            </h3>
                            <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '14px' }}>
                                <div>
                                    <label style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 600, color: '#475569', marginBottom: '6px' }}>예약자 성함</label>
                                    <input
                                        type="text"
                                        value={guestName}
                                        onChange={(e) => setGuestName(e.target.value)}
                                        placeholder="홍길동"
                                        style={{ width: '100%', padding: '10px 12px', borderRadius: '8px', border: '1px solid #cbd5e1', fontSize: '14px', boxSizing: 'border-box' }}
                                        required
                                    />
                                </div>
                                <div>
                                    <label style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 600, color: '#475569', marginBottom: '6px' }}>연락처</label>
                                    <input
                                        type="tel"
                                        value={phone}
                                        onChange={(e) => setPhone(e.target.value)}
                                        placeholder="010-1234-5678"
                                        style={{ width: '100%', padding: '10px 12px', borderRadius: '8px', border: '1px solid #cbd5e1', fontSize: '14px', boxSizing: 'border-box' }}
                                        required
                                    />
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* 우측: 숙소 요약 및 결제 위젯 영역 */}
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '20px' }}>
                        <div style={{ backgroundColor: '#ffffff', borderRadius: '16px', padding: '24px', border: '1px solid #e2e8f0', boxShadow: '0 4px 16px rgba(0,0,0,0.04)' }}>
                            <h4 style={{ fontSize: '14px', color: '#0f766e', fontWeight: 700, margin: '0 0 6px 0' }}>숙소 정보</h4>
                            <strong style={{ fontSize: '18px', color: '#0f172a', display: 'block', marginBottom: '6px' }}>{roomState.stayTitle}</strong>
                            <p style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: '#64748b', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '4px', margin: '0 0 16px 0' }}>
                                <MapPin size={14} /> {roomState.stayAddr}
                            </p>

                            <div style={{ borderTop: '1px solid #f1f5f9', paddingTop: '16px', marginBottom: '16px' }}>
                                <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '12px', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                                    {roomState.roomImage && (
                                        <img
                                            src={roomState.roomImage}
                                            alt={roomState.roomTitle}
                                            style={{ width: '64px', height: '64px', borderRadius: '8px', objectFit: 'cover' }}
                                        />
                                    )}
                                    <div>
                                        <div style={{ fontSize: '15px', fontWeight: 700, color: '#0f172a' }}>{roomState.roomTitle}</div>
                                        <div style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: '#64748b' }}>1박 기준 {money(roomState.pricePerNight)}</div>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                            </div>

                            <div id="payment-widget" style={{ margin: '10px -10px' }} />
                            <div id="agreement" style={{ margin: '0 -10px 10px -10px' }} />

                            <button
                                type="submit"
                                disabled={nights <= 0}
                                style={{
                                    width: '100%',
                                    marginTop: '8px',
                                    padding: '14px 0',
                                    backgroundColor: '#123f4d',
                                    color: '#ffffff',
                                    border: 'none',
                                    borderRadius: '10px',
                                    fontWeight: 700,
                                    fontSize: '15px',
                                    cursor: nights <= 0 ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
                                    transition: 'background-color 0.2s'
                                }}
                            >
                                {money(totalPrice)} 안전 결제하기
                            </button>
                            <div style={{ marginTop: '12px', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', gap: '6px', fontSize: '12px', color: '#94a3b8' }}>
                                <ShieldCheck size={14} color="#0f766e" /> 안전 결제 및 실시간 재고 보장
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </form>
            </main>
            <Footer />
        </div>
    );
}


// ==========================================
// File: src/pages/reservation/MyReservationsPage.tsx
// ==========================================
import { useEffect, useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import axios from 'axios';
import { CalendarX } from 'lucide-react';
import { Footer } from '../../components/common/Footer.tsx';
import { PageHeader} from "../../components/common/PageHeader.tsx";
import { MyReservationCard} from "../../components/reservation/MyReservationCard.tsx";
import { ReviewWriteModal} from "../../components/reservation/ReviewWriteModal.tsx";

export interface MyReservationItem {
    reservationId: number;
    status: 'COMPLETED' | 'CANCELED' | string;
    checkInDate: string;
    checkOutDate: string;
    totalPrice: number;
    createdAt: string;

    contentId: string;
    stayTitle: string;
    lodgingType: string;
    addr: string;
    tel: string;
    firstImage: string;
    checkinTime: string;
    checkoutTime: string;

    roomId: number;
    roomTitle: string;
    roomBaseCount: string;
    roomMaxCount: string;

    hasReview: boolean;
}

type FilterTab = 'ALL' | 'UPCOMING' | 'PAST' | 'CANCELED';

export function MyReservationsPage() {
    const navigate = useNavigate();

    const [reservations, setReservations] = useState<MyReservationItem[]>([]);
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
    const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<FilterTab>('ALL');
    const [cancelingId, setCancelingId] = useState<number | null>(null);
    const [reviewTarget, setReviewTarget] = useState<MyReservationItem | null>(null);

    const todayStr = useMemo(() => new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0], []);

    const loadMyReservations = () => {
        const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
        if (!token) {
            alert('로그인이 필요한 페이지입니다.');
            navigate('/login');
            return;
        }

        setLoading(true);
        axios.get<MyReservationItem[]>('/api/v1/reservations/my', {
            headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }
        })
            .then((res) => setReservations(res.data))
            .catch((err) => {
                console.error('예약 내역 조회 실패:', err);
                alert('예약 내역을 불러오지 못했습니다.');
            })
            .finally(() => setLoading(false));
    };

    useEffect(() => {
        window.scrollTo(0, 0);
        loadMyReservations();
    }, []);

    const getReservationCategory = (item: MyReservationItem): 'UPCOMING' | 'PAST' | 'CANCELED' => {
        if (item.status === 'CANCELED') return 'CANCELED';
        if (item.checkOutDate > todayStr) return 'UPCOMING';
        return 'PAST';
    };

    const counts = useMemo(() => {
        const result = { ALL: reservations.length, UPCOMING: 0, PAST: 0, CANCELED: 0 };
        reservations.forEach((r) => {
            const cat = getReservationCategory(r);
            result[cat]++;
        });
        return result;
    }, [reservations, todayStr]);

    const filteredList = useMemo(() => {
        if (activeTab === 'ALL') return reservations;
        return reservations.filter((r) => getReservationCategory(r) === activeTab);
    }, [reservations, activeTab, todayStr]);

    const handleCancelReservation = async (item: MyReservationItem) => {
        const confirmCancel = window.confirm(
            `[${item.stayTitle} - ${item.roomTitle}]\n정말 예약을 취소하시겠습니까? 취소 시 객실 재고가 즉시 복구됩니다.`
        );
        if (!confirmCancel) return;

        const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
        setCancelingId(item.reservationId);

        try {
            await axios.patch(`/api/v1/reservations/${item.reservationId}/cancel`, {}, {
                headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }
            });
            alert('예약이 정상적으로 취소되었습니다.');
            setReservations((prev) =>
                prev.map((r) =>
                    r.reservationId === item.reservationId ? { ...r, status: 'CANCELED' } : r
                )
            );
        } catch (err: any) {
            alert(err.response?.data?.message || err.response?.data || '예약 취소 처리 중 오류가 발생했습니다.');
        } finally {
            setCancelingId(null);
        }
    };

    return (
        <div style={{ backgroundColor: '#f8fafc', minHeight: '100vh', display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column' }}>
            {/* 1. 공통 상단 헤더 */}
            <PageHeader title="예매한 숙소 조회" />

            <main className="container" style={{ maxWidth: '920px', margin: '28px auto', padding: '0 20px', flex: 1, width: '100%' }}>
                <div style={{ marginBottom: '20px' }}>
                    <h1 style={{ fontSize: '22px', fontWeight: 800, color: '#0f172a', margin: '0 0 6px 0' }}>내 숙소 예약 내역</h1>
                    <p style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: '#64748b', margin: 0 }}>
                        예약하신 제주 숙소의 일정과 상세 정보를 확인하고 관리할 수 있습니다.
                    </p>
                </div>

                {/* 2. 상단 필터 탭 */}
                <div style={{
                    display: 'flex',
                    gap: '8px',
                    marginBottom: '24px',
                    borderBottom: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                    paddingBottom: '12px',
                    overflowX: 'auto'
                }}>
                    {[
                        { key: 'ALL', label: '전체 내역', count: counts.ALL },
                        { key: 'UPCOMING', label: '이용 예정', count: counts.UPCOMING },
                        { key: 'PAST', label: '이용 완료', count: counts.PAST },
                        { key: 'CANCELED', label: '취소됨', count: counts.CANCELED },
                    ].map((tab) => {
                        const isActive = activeTab === tab.key;
                        return (
                            <button
                                key={tab.key}
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => setActiveTab(tab.key as FilterTab)}
                                style={{
                                    padding: '8px 16px',
                                    borderRadius: '20px',
                                    border: isActive ? '1px solid #123f4d' : '1px solid #cbd5e1',
                                    backgroundColor: isActive ? '#123f4d' : '#ffffff',
                                    color: isActive ? '#ffffff' : '#475569',
                                    fontSize: '13px',
                                    fontWeight: 600,
                                    cursor: 'pointer',
                                    display: 'flex',
                                    alignItems: 'center',
                                    gap: '6px',
                                    whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
                                    transition: 'all 0.15s ease'
                                }}
                            >
                                <span>{tab.label}</span>
                                <span style={{
                                    padding: '1px 7px',
                                    borderRadius: '10px',
                                    fontSize: '11px',
                                    backgroundColor: isActive ? 'rgba(255,255,255,0.2)' : '#f1f5f9',
                                    color: isActive ? '#ffffff' : '#64748b'
                                }}>
                                    {tab.count}
                                </span>
                            </button>
                        );
                    })}
                </div>

                {/* 3. 예약 리스트 렌더링 */}
                {loading ? (
                    <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', padding: '80px 0', color: '#94a3b8' }}>
                        예약 내역을 불러오는 중입니다...
                    </div>
                ) : filteredList.length === 0 ? (
                    <div style={{
                        textAlign: 'center',
                        padding: '80px 20px',
                        backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                        borderRadius: '16px',
                        border: '1px dashed #cbd5e1'
                    }}>
                        <CalendarX size={44} color="#cbd5e1" style={{ marginBottom: '14px' }} />
                        <h3 style={{ fontSize: '16px', color: '#475569', margin: '0 0 8px 0' }}>
                            해당하는 예약 내역이 없습니다.
                        </h3>
                        <p style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: '#94a3b8', margin: '0 0 20px 0' }}>
                            마음에 드는 제주 숙소를 찾아 새로운 여행을 계획해 보세요.
                        </p>
                        <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => navigate('/')}
                            style={{
                                padding: '10px 22px',
                                backgroundColor: '#123f4d',
                                color: '#ffffff',
                                border: 'none',
                                borderRadius: '24px',
                                fontSize: '13px',
                                fontWeight: 600,
                                cursor: 'pointer'
                            }}
                        >
                            숙소 둘러보기
                        </button>
                    </div>
                ) : (
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '18px' }}>
                        {filteredList.map((item) => (
                            <MyReservationCard
                                key={item.reservationId}
                                item={item}
                                category={getReservationCategory(item)}
                                todayStr={todayStr}
                                canceling={cancelingId === item.reservationId}
                                onCancel={handleCancelReservation}
                                onOpenReview={(target) => setReviewTarget(target)}
                            />
                        ))}
                    </div>
                )}
            </main>

            <Footer />

            {/* 4. 분리된 리뷰 작성 모달 */}
            <ReviewWriteModal
                target={reviewTarget}
                onClose={() => setReviewTarget(null)}
                onSuccess={loadMyReservations}
            />
        </div>
    );
}


// ==========================================
// File: src/pages/tour/TourWishlistPage.tsx
// ==========================================
import React, { useEffect, useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Search, Map as MapIcon, Compass } from 'lucide-react';
import { fetchMyTourWishlist, toggleTourLike, type TourSummary } from '../../api/tours.ts';
import { Footer } from '../../components/common/Footer.tsx';
import { TourMapModal } from '../../components/tour/TourMapModal.tsx';
import { PageHeader} from "../../components/common/PageHeader.tsx";
import { TourWishlistCard} from "../../components/tour/TourWishlistCard.tsx";

export function TourWishlistPage() {
    const navigate = useNavigate();

    const [wishlist, setWishlist] = useState<TourSummary[]>([]);
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
    const [regionFilter, setRegionFilter] = useState<'ALL' | 'JEJU' | 'SEOGWIPO'>('ALL');
    const [searchKeyword, setSearchKeyword] = useState('');
    const [removingIds, setRemovingIds] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());

    // 뒤로가기로 돌아왔을 때도 찜한 관광지 지도 모달 상태 유지
    const [isMapOpen, setIsMapOpen] = useState<boolean>(() => {
        return sessionStorage.getItem('wishlist_tour_map_open') === 'true';
    });

    useEffect(() => {
        sessionStorage.setItem('wishlist_tour_map_open', String(isMapOpen));
    }, [isMapOpen]);

    const loadTourWishlist = () => {
        const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
        if (!token) {
            alert('로그인이 필요한 페이지입니다.');
            navigate('/login');
            return;
        }

        setLoading(true);
        fetchMyTourWishlist()
            .then((data) => setWishlist(data))
            .catch((err) => {
                console.error('관광지 위시리스트 조회 실패:', err);
                alert('관광지 위시리스트를 불러오지 못했습니다.');
            })
            .finally(() => setLoading(false));
    };

    useEffect(() => {
        window.scrollTo(0, 0);
        loadTourWishlist();
    }, []);

    const filteredList = useMemo(() => {
        return wishlist.filter((item) => {
            const addr = `${item.addr1 || ''} ${item.addr2 || ''}`;
            if (regionFilter === 'JEJU' && !addr.includes('제주시')) return false;
            if (regionFilter === 'SEOGWIPO' && !addr.includes('서귀포')) return false;

            if (searchKeyword.trim()) {
                const kw = searchKeyword.trim().toLowerCase();
                const matchTitle = item.title?.toLowerCase().includes(kw);
                const matchAddr = addr.toLowerCase().includes(kw);
                if (!matchTitle && !matchAddr) return false;
            }
            return true;
        });
    }, [wishlist, regionFilter, searchKeyword]);

    const handleRemoveLike = async (e: React.MouseEvent, contentId: string) => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        if (removingIds.has(contentId)) return;

        try {
            await toggleTourLike(contentId);
            setRemovingIds((prev) => new Set(prev).add(contentId));

            setTimeout(() => {
                setWishlist((prev) => prev.filter((item) => item.contentId !== contentId));
                setRemovingIds((prev) => {
                    const next = new Set(prev);
                    next.delete(contentId);
                    return next;
                });
            }, 350);
        } catch {
            alert('위시리스트 삭제 중 오류가 발생했습니다.');
        }
    };

    return (
        <div style={{ backgroundColor: '#f8fafc', minHeight: '100vh', display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column' }}>
            {/* 1. 공통 상단 헤더 */}
            <PageHeader
                title="관광지 위시리스트"
                rightElement={
                    wishlist.length > 0 ? (
                        <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => setIsMapOpen(true)}
                            style={{
                                display: 'inline-flex',
                                alignItems: 'center',
                                gap: '6px',
                                padding: '8px 14px',
                                backgroundColor: '#0f766e',
                                color: '#ffffff',
                                border: 'none',
                                borderRadius: '20px',
                                fontSize: '12px',
                                fontWeight: 700,
                                cursor: 'pointer',
                                boxShadow: '0 2px 8px rgba(15, 118, 110, 0.2)'
                            }}
                        >
                            <MapIcon size={14} /> 찜한 관광지 지도 보기 ({filteredList.length})
                        </button>
                    ) : undefined
                }
            />

            <main className="container" style={{ maxWidth: '1120px', margin: '28px auto', padding: '0 20px', flex: 1, width: '100%' }}>
                {/* 2. 타이틀 및 필터 바 */}
                <div style={{
                    display: 'flex',
                    justifyContent: 'space-between',
                    alignItems: 'flex-end',
                    flexWrap: 'wrap',
                    gap: '16px',
                    marginBottom: '24px',
                    paddingBottom: '16px',
                    borderBottom: '1px solid #e2e8f0'
                }}>
                    <div>
                        <span style={{ fontSize: '12px', fontWeight: 800, color: '#0f766e', letterSpacing: '0.05em', display: 'block', marginBottom: '4px' }}>
                            MY JEJU ATTRACTIONS
                        </span>
                        <h1 style={{ fontSize: '24px', fontWeight: 800, color: '#0f172a', margin: '0 0 6px 0' }}>
                            내가 찜한 제주 명소 <span style={{ color: '#0f766e' }}>{wishlist.length}</span>
                        </h1>
                        <p style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: '#64748b', margin: 0 }}>
                            저장해 둔 관광지를 확인하고 나만의 제주 여행 동선을 계획해 보세요.
                        </p>
                    </div>

                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '10px', flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
                        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '6px' }}>
                            {[
                                { key: 'ALL', label: '전체' },
                                { key: 'JEJU', label: '제주시' },
                                { key: 'SEOGWIPO', label: '서귀포시' },
                            ].map((tab) => {
                                const active = regionFilter === tab.key;
                                return (
                                    <button
                                        key={tab.key}
                                        type="button"
                                        onClick={() => setRegionFilter(tab.key as 'ALL' | 'JEJU' | 'SEOGWIPO')}
                                        style={{
                                            padding: '7px 14px',
                                            borderRadius: '20px',
                                            border: active ? '1px solid #123f4d' : '1px solid #cbd5e1',
                                            backgroundColor: active ? '#123f4d' : '#ffffff',
                                            color: active ? '#ffffff' : '#475569',
                                            fontSize: '12px',
                                            fontWeight: 600,
                                            cursor: 'pointer',
                                        }}
                                    >
                                        {tab.label}
                                    </button>
                                );
                            })}
                        </div>

                        <div style={{ position: 'relative' }}>
                            <input
                                type="text"
                                value={searchKeyword}
                                onChange={(e) => setSearchKeyword(e.target.value)}
                                placeholder="관광지명 또는 주소 검색"
                                style={{
                                    padding: '8px 14px 8px 34px',
                                    borderRadius: '20px',
                                    border: '1px solid #cbd5e1',
                                    backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                                    fontSize: '13px',
                                    width: '200px',
                                    outline: 'none'
                                }}
                            />
                            <Search size={15} color="#94a3b8" style={{ position: 'absolute', left: '12px', top: '9px' }} />
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>

                {/* 3. 본문 그리드 */}
                {loading ? (
                    <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', padding: '80px 0', color: '#94a3b8' }}>
                        찜한 관광지 목록을 불러오는 중입니다...
                    </div>
                ) : wishlist.length === 0 ? (
                    <div style={{
                        textAlign: 'center',
                        padding: '80px 20px',
                        backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                        borderRadius: '16px',
                        border: '1px dashed #cbd5e1'
                    }}>
                        <Compass size={48} color="#cbd5e1" style={{ marginBottom: '14px' }} />
                        <h3 style={{ fontSize: '17px', color: '#334155', margin: '0 0 8px 0' }}>
                            아직 찜한 관광지가 없습니다.
                        </h3>
                        <p style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: '#94a3b8', margin: '0 0 22px 0' }}>
                            홈페이지 상단의 [관광 지도]에서 가고 싶은 제주 명소를 찾아 하트를 눌러보세요!
                        </p>
                        <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => navigate('/')}
                            style={{
                                padding: '11px 24px',
                                backgroundColor: '#123f4d',
                                color: '#ffffff',
                                border: 'none',
                                borderRadius: '24px',
                                fontSize: '13px',
                                fontWeight: 600,
                                cursor: 'pointer'
                            }}
                        >
                            메인으로 돌아가기
                        </button>
                    </div>
                ) : filteredList.length === 0 ? (
                    <div style={{
                        textAlign: 'center',
                        padding: '60px 20px',
                        backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                        borderRadius: '16px',
                        border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                        color: '#64748b',
                        fontSize: '14px'
                    }}>
                        검색 조건에 일치하는 관광지가 없습니다.
                    </div>
                ) : (
                    <div className="stay-grid">
                        {filteredList.map((tour) => (
                            <TourWishlistCard
                                key={tour.contentId}
                                tour={tour}
                                isRemoving={removingIds.has(tour.contentId)}
                                onRemoveLike={handleRemoveLike}
                            />
                        ))}
                    </div>
                )}
            </main>

            <Footer />

            <TourMapModal
                isOpen={isMapOpen}
                onClose={() => setIsMapOpen(false)}
                tours={filteredList}
                storageKeyPrefix="wishlist_tour_map"
            />
        </div>
    );
}


// ==========================================
// File: src/pages/tour/TourDetailPage.tsx
// ==========================================
import { useEffect, useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import { useParams, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import {
    MapPin,
    Phone,
    Clock,
    Car,
    Heart,
    CalendarOff,
    Ticket,
    CalendarCheck,
    CircleDollarSign,
    Maximize2
} from 'lucide-react';
import { fetchTourDetail, toggleTourLike, type TourDetail } from '../../api/tours.ts';
import { Footer } from '../../components/common/Footer.tsx';
import { KakaoTourMap } from '../../components/tour/KakaoTourMap.tsx';
import { PageHeader} from "../../components/common/PageHeader.tsx";
import { DetailPhotoGallery } from '../../components/common/DetailPhotoGallery.tsx';
import { LocationMapPopupModal} from "../../components/common/LocationMapPopupModal.tsx";

function cleanHtmlText(text?: string): string {
    if (!text) return '';
    return text
        .replace(/<br\s*\/?>/gi, '\n')
        .replace(/&nbsp;/gi, ' ')
        .replace(/<[^>]*>?/gm, '')
        .trim();
}

export function TourDetailPage() {
    const { contentId } = useParams<{ contentId: string }>();
    const navigate = useNavigate();

    const [tour, setTour] = useState<TourDetail | null>(null);
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
    const [isLiked, setIsLiked] = useState(false);
    const [isMapPopupOpen, setIsMapPopupOpen] = useState(false);

    useEffect(() => {
        window.scrollTo(0, 0);
    }, [contentId]);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (!contentId) return;
        setLoading(true);
        fetchTourDetail(contentId)
            .then((data) => setTour(data))
            .catch((err) => {
                console.error(err);
                alert('관광지 정보를 불러오지 못했습니다.');
                navigate('/');
            })
            .finally(() => setLoading(false));
    }, [contentId, navigate]);

    useEffect(() => {
        const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
        if (token && contentId) {
            fetch(`/api/v1/tours/likes/${contentId}/status`, {
                headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }
            })
                .then((res) => (res.ok ? res.json() : false))
                .then((data) => setIsLiked(Boolean(data)))
                .catch(() => setIsLiked(false));
        }
    }, [contentId]);

    const handleToggleLike = async () => {
        const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
        if (!token) {
            alert('로그인이 필요한 기능입니다.');
            navigate('/login');
            return;
        }
        if (!contentId) return;

        try {
            const res = await toggleTourLike(contentId);
            setIsLiked(res.liked);
            alert(res.message);
        } catch {
            alert('위시리스트 처리 중 오류가 발생했습니다.');
        }
    };

    const allGalleryImages = useMemo(() => {
        if (!tour) return [];
        const urls: string[] = [];
        const seen = new Set<string>();

        if (tour.firstImage) {
            urls.push(tour.firstImage);
            seen.add(tour.firstImage);
        }
        if (tour.images && tour.images.length > 0) {
            tour.images.forEach((img) => {
                if (img.originImgUrl && !seen.has(img.originImgUrl)) {
                    urls.push(img.originImgUrl);
                    seen.add(img.originImgUrl);
                }
            });
        }
        return urls;
    }, [tour]);

    if (loading) {
        return (
            <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', padding: '120px 0', color: '#64748b' }}>
                관광지 상세 정보를 불러오는 중입니다...
            </div>
        );
    }

    if (!tour) return null;

    const fullAddress = `${tour.addr1 || ''} ${tour.addr2 || ''}`.trim();

    const infoItems = [
        { icon: <Clock size={16} color="#0f766e" />, label: '이용시간', value: cleanHtmlText(tour.useTime) },
        { icon: <CalendarOff size={16} color="#e11d48" />, label: '쉬는날 (휴무일)', value: cleanHtmlText(tour.restDate) },
        { icon: <Ticket size={16} color="#0f766e" />, label: '입장료 / 이용요금', value: cleanHtmlText(tour.useFee) },
        { icon: <Car size={16} color="#0f766e" />, label: '주차시설', value: cleanHtmlText(tour.parking) },
        { icon: <CircleDollarSign size={16} color="#0f766e" />, label: '주차요금', value: cleanHtmlText(tour.parkingFee) },
        { icon: <Phone size={16} color="#0f766e" />, label: '문의 및 안내', value: cleanHtmlText(tour.tel) },
        { icon: <CalendarCheck size={16} color="#0f766e" />, label: '개장일', value: cleanHtmlText(tour.openDate) },
    ].filter((item) => item.value && item.value.length > 0);

    return (
        <div style={{ backgroundColor: '#f8fafc', minHeight: '100vh' }}>
            {/* 1. 공통 상단 헤더 */}
            <PageHeader title={tour.title} titleSize="20px" />

            <main className="container" style={{ maxWidth: '1050px', margin: '24px auto', padding: '0 16px' }}>
                {/* 2. 공통 사진 갤러리 (5분할 + 전체사진 모달 + 1장 확대 팝업 자동 포함) */}
                <DetailPhotoGallery
                    images={allGalleryImages}
                    emptyText="등록된 관광지 사진이 없습니다."
                    modalTitle="관광지 사진"
                />

                {/* 3. 관광지 기본 헤더 및 이용 안내 정보 */}
                <section style={{
                    backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                    borderRadius: '16px',
                    padding: '28px',
                    border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                    marginBottom: '24px',
                    position: 'relative'
                }}>
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={handleToggleLike}
                        style={{
                            position: 'absolute',
                            top: '24px',
                            right: '28px',
                            background: 'none',
                            border: 'none',
                            cursor: 'pointer',
                            padding: '6px',
                            display: 'flex',
                            alignItems: 'center',
                            justifyContent: 'center',
                            transition: 'transform 0.15s ease',
                        }}
                        onMouseEnter={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.transform = 'scale(1.1)')}
                        onMouseLeave={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.transform = 'scale(1.0)')}
                        aria-label="관광지 찜하기"
                    >
                        <Heart
                            size={38}
                            fill={isLiked ? '#ef4444' : 'none'}
                            color={isLiked ? '#ef4444' : '#94a3b8'}
                            strokeWidth={1.7}
                        />
                    </button>

                    <span style={{
                        fontSize: '12px',
                        fontWeight: 700,
                        color: '#0f766e',
                        backgroundColor: '#ccfbf1',
                        padding: '4px 10px',
                        borderRadius: '6px'
                    }}>
                        제주 관광명소
                    </span>

                    <h1 style={{ fontSize: '26px', color: '#0f172a', margin: '12px 0 8px 0', paddingRight: '60px' }}>
                        {tour.title}
                    </h1>

                    {fullAddress && (
                        <p style={{
                            display: 'flex',
                            alignItems: 'center',
                            gap: '6px',
                            color: '#64748b',
                            fontSize: '14px',
                            margin: '0 0 20px 0'
                        }}>
                            <MapPin size={17} color="#0f766e" />
                            <span>{fullAddress} {tour.zipcode && `(${tour.zipcode})`}</span>
                        </p>
                    )}

                    {infoItems.length > 0 && (
                        <div style={{
                            display: 'grid',
                            gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fit, minmax(260px, 1fr))',
                            gap: '14px',
                            padding: '18px',
                            backgroundColor: '#f8fafc',
                            borderRadius: '12px',
                            border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                        }}>
                            {infoItems.map((item, idx) => (
                                <div key={idx} style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'flex-start', gap: '10px' }}>
                                    <div style={{ marginTop: '2px', flexShrink: 0 }}>{item.icon}</div>
                                    <div style={{ fontSize: '13px', lineHeight: 1.5 }}>
                                        <span style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '11px', fontWeight: 700, color: '#64748b' }}>
                                            {item.label}
                                        </span>
                                        <span style={{ color: '#1e293b', fontWeight: 600, whiteSpace: 'pre-line' }}>
                                            {item.value}
                                        </span>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                            ))}
                        </div>
                    )}
                </section>

                {/* 4. 관광지 상세 개요 및 위치 지도 */}
                <section style={{
                    backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                    borderRadius: '16px',
                    padding: '28px',
                    border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                    marginBottom: '24px'
                }}>
                    <h2 style={{ fontSize: '18px', color: '#0f172a', marginBottom: '14px' }}>상세 소개</h2>
                    <p style={{
                        color: '#475569',
                        fontSize: '14px',
                        lineHeight: '1.8',
                        whiteSpace: 'pre-line',
                        margin: '0 0 28px 0'
                    }}>
                        {cleanHtmlText(tour.overview) || '등록된 상세 소개글이 없습니다.'}
                    </p>

                    {tour.mapX && tour.mapY && (
                        <>
                            <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', margin: '0 0 12px 0' }}>
                                <h3 style={{ fontSize: '16px', color: '#0f172a', margin: 0 }}>위치 안내</h3>
                                <button
                                    type="button"
                                    onClick={() => setIsMapPopupOpen(true)}
                                    style={{
                                        display: 'inline-flex',
                                        alignItems: 'center',
                                        gap: '5px',
                                        padding: '6px 12px',
                                        borderRadius: '20px',
                                        border: '1px solid #cbd5e1',
                                        backgroundColor: '#f8fafc',
                                        color: '#123f4d',
                                        fontSize: '12px',
                                        fontWeight: 700,
                                        cursor: 'pointer'
                                    }}
                                >
                                    <Maximize2 size={13} /> 지도 크게 보기
                                </button>
                            </div>

                            <div
                                onClick={() => setIsMapPopupOpen(true)}
                                style={{
                                    position: 'relative',
                                    borderRadius: '12px',
                                    overflow: 'hidden',
                                    border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                                    cursor: 'pointer'
                                }}
                            >
                                <KakaoTourMap
                                    tours={[tour]}
                                    mode="detail"
                                    level={4}
                                    height="360px"
                                    zoomable={false}
                                />
                                <div
                                    style={{
                                        position: 'absolute',
                                        top: '12px',
                                        left: '12px',
                                        backgroundColor: 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.78)',
                                        backdropFilter: 'blur(4px)',
                                        color: '#ffffff',
                                        padding: '6px 12px',
                                        borderRadius: '20px',
                                        fontSize: '12px',
                                        fontWeight: 600,
                                        display: 'inline-flex',
                                        alignItems: 'center',
                                        gap: '6px',
                                        pointerEvents: 'none',
                                        zIndex: 5,
                                    }}
                                >
                                    <Maximize2 size={13} color="#5eead4" />
                                    지도를 클릭하면 팝업창에서 마우스 휠 확대/축소가 가능합니다
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </>
                    )}
                </section>
            </main>

            <Footer />

            {/* 5. 공통 지도 크게 보기 팝업 모달 */}
            {tour.mapX && tour.mapY && (
                <LocationMapPopupModal
                    isOpen={isMapPopupOpen}
                    onClose={() => setIsMapPopupOpen(false)}
                    eyebrow="ATTRACTION LOCATION MAP"
                    title={tour.title}
                >
                    <KakaoTourMap
                        tours={[tour]}
                        mode="detail"
                        level={4}
                        height="100%"
                        zoomable={true}
                    />
                </LocationMapPopupModal>
            )}
        </div>
    );
}


// ==========================================
// File: src/pages/HomePage.tsx
// ==========================================
import React, { useState, useEffect, useMemo } from "react";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { ArrowRight, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight, Map as MapIcon } from "lucide-react";
import { BookingModal } from "../components/stay/BookingModal.tsx";
import { useHeroSlider } from "../hooks/useHeroSlider";
import { useReservation } from "../hooks/useReservation";
import { useStays } from "../hooks/useStays";
import { destinations, regions, types, fetchStays } from "../data/stays";
import { Footer } from "../components/common/Footer.tsx";
import { HostApplyBanner } from "../components/home/HostApplyBanner.tsx";
import { StayMapModal } from "../components/stay/StayMapModal.tsx";
import { TourMapModal } from "../components/tour/TourMapModal.tsx";
import { TourSliderSection } from "../components/tour/TourSliderSection.tsx";
import { StayCard } from "../components/stay/StayCard.tsx";
import { HomeNavbar } from "../components/home/HomeNavbar.tsx";
import { StaySearchBar} from "../components/home/StaySearchBar.tsx";
import type { Stay } from "../types/stay";
import { fetchMyWishlist, toggleAccommodationLike } from "../api/stays";
import { fetchTours, type TourSummary } from "../api/tours";

const heroTitles = [
    "제주에 머무는 시간,\n여행이 되다",
    "바다와 더 가까운\n제주의 하루",
    "오늘의 제주에서\n쉬어 가세요",
];

export function HomePage() {
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const {
        region,
        setRegion,
        type,
        setType,
        query,
        setQuery,
        result,
        loading,
        apiOnline,
        selectRegion,
        search,
    } = useStays();

    const todayStr = useMemo(() => new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0], []);
    const tomorrowStr = useMemo(() => {
        const d = new Date();
        d.setDate(d.getDate() + 1);
        return d.toISOString().split('T')[0];
    }, []);

    const [checkIn, setCheckIn] = useState(todayStr);
    const [checkOut, setCheckOut] = useState(tomorrowStr);
    const [guests, setGuests] = useState(2);
    const [visibleCount, setVisibleCount] = useState(18);
    const [isLoggedIn, setIsLoggedIn] = useState(() => Boolean(localStorage.getItem('accessToken')));

    // 지도 모달 상태
    const [modalStays, setModalStays] = useState<Stay[]>([]);
    const [isMapModalOpen, setIsMapModalOpen] = useState(false);

    // 관광지 슬라이더(9개) 및 관광 지도 모달 상태 (뒤로가기 시 유지)
    const [homeTours, setHomeTours] = useState<TourSummary[]>([]);
    const [modalTours, setModalTours] = useState<TourSummary[]>([]);
    const [isTourMapModalOpen, setIsTourMapModalOpen] = useState<boolean>(() => {
        return sessionStorage.getItem('home_tour_map_open') === 'true';
    });

    useEffect(() => {
        sessionStorage.setItem('home_tour_map_open', String(isTourMapModalOpen));
    }, [isTourMapModalOpen]);

    useEffect(() => {
        fetchTours()
            .then((data) => {
                setModalTours(data);
                const withImages = data.filter((t) => t.firstImage);
                const pool = withImages.length >= 9 ? withImages : data;
                const shuffled = [...pool].sort(() => Math.random() - 0.5);
                setHomeTours(shuffled.slice(0, 9));
            })
            .catch((err) => console.error('관광지 목록 로딩 실패:', err));
    }, []);

    const handleMapSearch = async () => {
        const currentScrollY = window.scrollY;
        search(checkIn, checkOut, guests);
        window.scrollTo(0, currentScrollY);

        try {
            const filteredStays = await fetchStays(region, type, query, checkIn, checkOut, guests);
            setModalStays(filteredStays);
        } catch {
            setModalStays(result);
        }
        setIsMapModalOpen(true);
    };

    const handleOpenTourMap = async () => {
        if (modalTours.length === 0) {
            try {
                const allTours = await fetchTours();
                setHomeTours(allTours.slice(0, 9));
                setModalTours(allTours);
            } catch {
                setModalTours([]);
            }
        }
        setIsTourMapModalOpen(true);
    };

    const handleLogout = () => {
        localStorage.removeItem('accessToken');
        localStorage.removeItem('refreshToken');
        setIsLoggedIn(false);
        alert('로그아웃되었습니다.');
        navigate('/');
    };

    const [likedContentIds, setLikedContentIds] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());

    useEffect(() => {
        const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
        if (token && isLoggedIn) {
            fetchMyWishlist()
                .then((data) => setLikedContentIds(new Set(data.map((item) => item.contentId))))
                .catch((err) => console.error("위시리스트 로딩 실패:", err));
        } else {
            setLikedContentIds(new Set());
        }
    }, [isLoggedIn]);

    const handleToggleWishlist = async (e: React.MouseEvent, contentId: string) => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        if (!isLoggedIn) {
            alert("로그인이 필요한 기능입니다.");
            navigate("/login");
            return;
        }
        try {
            const res = await toggleAccommodationLike(contentId);
            setLikedContentIds((prev) => {
                const next = new Set(prev);
                if (res.liked) next.add(contentId);
                else next.delete(contentId);
                return next;
            });
        } catch {
            alert("위시리스트 처리 중 오류가 발생했습니다.");
        }
    };

    useEffect(() => {
        setVisibleCount(18);
    }, [region, type, query]);

    const { slide, previous, next } = useHeroSlider();
    const reservation = useReservation(apiOnline);
    const { open } = reservation;

    return (
        <>
            {/* 1. 분리된 상단 네비게이션 바 */}
            <HomeNavbar
                isLoggedIn={isLoggedIn}
                onLogout={handleLogout}
                onOpenTourMap={handleOpenTourMap}
            />

            <main id="top">
                {/* 2. 히어로 배너 슬라이더 */}
                <section className={`hero hero-${slide}`}>
                    <div className="hero-image" />
                    <div className="hero-shade" />
                    <div className="container hero-inner">
                        <div className="hero-copy">
                            <div className="eyebrow light">ONLY JEJU · STAY & TRAVEL</div>
                            <h1>{heroTitles[slide]}</h1>
                            <p>가고 싶은 여행지를 찾고, 마음에 드는 숙소를 만나보세요.</p>
                            <a className="hero-link" href="#stays">
                                숙소 둘러보기 <ArrowRight size={18} />
                            </a>
                        </div>
                        <div className="slide-control">
                            <button aria-label="이전 배너" onClick={previous}>
                                <ChevronLeft size={18} />
                            </button>
                            <span>
                                0{slide + 1} <i /> 03
                            </span>
                            <button aria-label="다음 배너" onClick={next}>
                                <ChevronRight size={18} />
                            </button>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </section>

                {/* 3. 분리된 숙소 검색 패널 */}
                <StaySearchBar
                    todayStr={todayStr}
                    checkIn={checkIn}
                    checkOut={checkOut}
                    guests={guests}
                    query={query}
                    onCheckInChange={setCheckIn}
                    onCheckOutChange={setCheckOut}
                    onGuestsChange={setGuests}
                    onQueryChange={setQuery}
                    onSearchSubmit={() => search(checkIn, checkOut, guests)}
                    onMapSearch={handleMapSearch}
                />

                {/* 4. 추천 여행지 */}
                <section id="destinations" className="section container">
                    <div className="section-top">
                        <div>
                            <span className="eyebrow">DISCOVER JEJU</span>
                            <h2>제주, 어디에 머물까요?</h2>
                            <p>여행의 분위기를 정하면 숙소 고르기도 쉬워져요.</p>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <div className="destination-grid">
                        {destinations.map((d) => (
                            <button
                                key={d.name}
                                className={`destination ${d.className}`}
                                onClick={() => selectRegion(d.name)}
                            >
                                <span className="destination-image" />
                                <span className="destination-content">
                                    <small>JEJU ISLAND</small>
                                    <strong>{d.name}</strong>
                                    <span>
                                        {d.note} <ArrowRight size={17} />
                                    </span>
                                </span>
                            </button>
                        ))}
                    </div>
                </section>

                {/* 5. 관광지 추천 슬라이더 */}
                <section
                    id="travel"
                    className="section container"
                    style={{
                        paddingTop: 0,
                        scrollMarginTop: '180px' // 💡 상단 고정 헤더와 검색창 높이만큼 스크롤 오프셋 확보
                    }}
                >
                    <TourSliderSection
                        tours={homeTours}
                        headerRender={
                            <div>
                                <span className="eyebrow">TRAVEL JEJU</span>
                                <h2 style={{ fontSize: '28px', fontWeight: 800, color: '#0f172a', margin: '4px 0 6px 0' }}>
                                    제주, 어디를 찾을까요?
                                </h2>
                                <p style={{ color: '#64748b', fontSize: '15px', margin: 0 }}>
                                    가고 싶은 관광지를 찾을 수 있어요.
                                </p>
                            </div>
                        }
                        footerRender={
                            <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={handleOpenTourMap}
                                style={{
                                    display: 'inline-flex',
                                    alignItems: 'center',
                                    gap: '8px',
                                    padding: '13px 32px',
                                    fontSize: '15px',
                                    fontWeight: 700,
                                    borderRadius: '30px',
                                    backgroundColor: '#0f766e',
                                    color: '#ffffff',
                                    border: 'none',
                                    cursor: 'pointer',
                                    boxShadow: '0 4px 14px rgba(15, 118, 110, 0.25)',
                                }}
                            >
                                <MapIcon size={17} />
                                더 많은 관광지를 둘러보세요
                                <ArrowRight size={16} />
                            </button>
                        }
                    />
                </section>

                {/* 6. 숙소 목록 */}
                <section id="stays" className="section stay-section">
                    <div className="container">
                        <div className="section-top">
                            <div>
                                <span className="eyebrow">STAY IN JEJU</span>
                                <h2>제주에서 쉬어 갈 곳</h2>
                                <p>
                                    {loading
                                        ? "숙소 정보를 불러오는 중입니다."
                                        : `${result.length}개의 숙소를 둘러보세요.`}
                                </p>
                            </div>
                            <span className="sample-note">
                                {!apiOnline && "화면 예시 숙소 · 실제 예약 준비 중"}
                            </span>
                        </div>

                        <div className="category-row">
                            <span>지역</span>
                            <div className="chips">
                                {regions.map((r) => (
                                    <button
                                        className={region === r ? "active" : ""}
                                        key={r}
                                        onClick={() => setRegion(r)}
                                    >
                                        {r}
                                    </button>
                                ))}
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        <div className="category-row">
                            <span>숙박 종류</span>
                            <div className="chips">
                                {types.map((t) => (
                                    <button
                                        className={type === t ? "active" : ""}
                                        key={t}
                                        onClick={() => setType(t)}
                                    >
                                        {t}
                                    </button>
                                ))}
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        <div className="stay-grid">
                            {result.slice(0, visibleCount).map((s) => (
                                <StayCard
                                    key={s.contentId}
                                    stay={s}
                                    isLiked={likedContentIds.has(s.contentId)}
                                    onCardClick={(stay) => open(stay)}
                                    onToggleLike={handleToggleWishlist}
                                />
                            ))}
                        </div>

                        {visibleCount < result.length && (
                            <div style={{ textAlign: "center", marginTop: "40px" }}>
                                <button
                                    type="button"
                                    onClick={() => setVisibleCount((prev) => prev + 18)}
                                    style={{
                                        padding: "12px 36px",
                                        fontSize: "16px",
                                        fontWeight: 600,
                                        borderRadius: "30px",
                                        backgroundColor: "#123f4d",
                                        color: "#ffffff",
                                        border: "none",
                                        cursor: "pointer",
                                    }}
                                >
                                    숙소 더보기 ({visibleCount} / {result.length})
                                </button>
                            </div>
                        )}

                        {!result.length && (
                            <div className="empty">
                                선택한 조건의 숙소가 없습니다. 다른 지역이나 숙박 종류를 골라 주세요.
                            </div>
                        )}
                    </div>
                </section>

                {/* 7. 하단 안내 배너 */}
                <section id="about" className="bottom-banner">
                    <div className="container">
                        <div>
                            <span className="eyebrow light">A LITTLE CLOSER TO JEJU</span>
                            <h2>당신의 속도로, 제주를 만나세요.</h2>
                            <p>머무는 곳에서 시작되는 여행의 좋은 순간들.</p>
                        </div>
                        <a href="#destinations">
                            여행지 둘러보기 <ArrowRight size={18} />
                        </a>
                    </div>
                </section>
            </main>

            <HostApplyBanner />
            <Footer />
            <BookingModal reservation={reservation} />

            <StayMapModal
                isOpen={isMapModalOpen}
                onClose={() => setIsMapModalOpen(false)}
                stays={modalStays}
            />

            <TourMapModal
                isOpen={isTourMapModalOpen}
                onClose={() => setIsTourMapModalOpen(false)}
                tours={modalTours}
            />
        </>
    );
}


// ==========================================
// File: src/main/java/org/example/hotelbooking/config/RedissonConfig.java
// ==========================================
package org.example.hotelbooking.config;

import org.redisson.Redisson;
import org.redisson.api.RedissonClient;
import org.redisson.config.Config;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;

@Configuration
public class RedissonConfig {

    private static final String REDIS_URL = "redis://127.0.0.1:6379";

    @Bean
    public RedissonClient redissonClient() {
        Config config = new Config();
        // 도커로 띄운 로컬 Redis 주소로 연결
        config.useSingleServer().setAddress(REDIS_URL);

        return Redisson.create(config);
    }
}

// File: src/main/java/org/example/hotelbooking/domain/like/repository/AccomLikeRepository.java
// ==========================================
package org.example.hotelbooking.domain.like.repository;

import org.example.hotelbooking.domain.accommodation.dto.StayResponseDto;
import org.example.hotelbooking.domain.like.entity.AccomLike;
import org.springframework.data.jpa.repository.JpaRepository;
import org.springframework.data.jpa.repository.Query;
import org.springframework.data.repository.query.Param;

import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;

public interface AccomLikeRepository extends JpaRepository<AccomLike, Long> {
    boolean existsByMember_MemberIdAndAccommodation_ContentId(Long memberId, String contentId);

    Optional<AccomLike> findByMember_MemberIdAndAccommodation_ContentId(Long memberId, String contentId);

    List<AccomLike> findByMember_MemberIdOrderByCreatedAtDesc(Long memberId);

    // 💡 날짜가 있으면 재고가 있는 찜 숙소만 필터링, 날짜가 없으면(CAST IS NULL) 전체 찜 숙소 반환
    @Query("SELECT new org.example.hotelbooking.domain.accommodation.dto.StayResponseDto(" +
            "a.contentId, a.host.memberId, a.title, a.lodgingType, a.tel, a.zipcode, a.addr, " +
            "a.checkinTime, a.checkoutTime, a.roomCount, a.roomType, a.parking, a.subFacility, " +
            "a.overview, a.firstImage, a.firstImage2, a.mapX, a.mapY, " +
            "COALESCE((SELECT MIN(r.pricePerNight) FROM Room r WHERE r.accommodation = a), 0), " +
            "COALESCE((SELECT SUM(ri.availableCount) FROM RoomInventory ri JOIN ri.room r " +
            "          WHERE r.accommodation = a " +
            "            AND ri.inventoryDate = :checkIn " +
            "            AND (:guests IS NULL OR :guests <= 0 OR CAST(COALESCE(r.roomMaxCount, '0') AS integer) >= :guests)), 0L)) " +
            "FROM AccomLike al " +
            "JOIN al.accommodation a " +
            "WHERE al.member.memberId = :memberId " +
            // 💡 1. 인원 조건
            "  AND (:guests IS NULL OR :guests <= 0 OR EXISTS (" +
            "       SELECT 1 FROM Room r " +
            "       WHERE r.accommodation = a " +
            "         AND CAST(COALESCE(r.roomMaxCount, '0') AS integer) >= :guests)) " +
            // 💡 2. 날짜 조건 (:stayDays = 0L 이면 전체 찜 숙소 그대로 반환!)
            "  AND (:stayDays = 0L OR EXISTS (" +
            "       SELECT 1 FROM Room r " +
            "       WHERE r.accommodation = a " +
            "         AND (:guests IS NULL OR :guests <= 0 OR CAST(COALESCE(r.roomMaxCount, '0') AS integer) >= :guests) " +
            "         AND (SELECT COUNT(ri) FROM RoomInventory ri " +
            "              WHERE ri.room = r " +
            "                AND ri.inventoryDate >= :checkIn " +
            "                AND ri.inventoryDate < :checkOut " +
            "                AND ri.availableCount > 0) = :stayDays)) " +
            "ORDER BY al.createdAt DESC")
    List<StayResponseDto> findWishlistStaysByMemberIdAndDates(
            @Param("memberId") Long memberId,
            @Param("checkIn") LocalDate checkIn,
            @Param("checkOut") LocalDate checkOut,
            @Param("stayDays") Long stayDays,
            @Param("guests") Integer guests
    );

    // 💡 숙소 삭제 시 해당 숙소를 찜한 내역 일괄 삭제
    void deleteByAccommodation_ContentId(String contentId);
}

// File: src/main/java/org/example/hotelbooking/domain/like/entity/AccomLike.java
// ==========================================
package org.example.hotelbooking.domain.like.entity;

import org.example.hotelbooking.domain.accommodation.entity.Accommodation;
import org.example.hotelbooking.domain.member.entity.Member;
import jakarta.persistence.*;
import lombok.*;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;

@Entity
@Getter
@NoArgsConstructor(access = AccessLevel.PROTECTED)
@Table(name = "ACCOM_LIKE")
public class AccomLike {

    @Id @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.IDENTITY)
    @Column(name = "LIKE_ID")
    private Long likeId;

    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY)
    @JoinColumn(name = "MEMBER_ID", nullable = false)
    private Member member;

    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY)
    @JoinColumn(name = "CONTENT_ID", nullable = false)
    private Accommodation accommodation;

    @Column(name = "CREATED_AT", updatable = false)
    private LocalDateTime createdAt;

    @PrePersist
    protected void onCreate() {
        this.createdAt = LocalDateTime.now();
    }
    @Builder
    public AccomLike(Member member, Accommodation accommodation) {
        this.member = member;
        this.accommodation = accommodation;
    }
}
